The rule comes after the guard-abort and infra rules, so a pipeline fault in the same failure keeps its class.
  - Pinned by `TestRebaseRecordingConflicts_TheLaneLandsItsOwnInboxItemWithMainsStampsKept`, `TestRebaseRecordingConflicts_TheCyclesConsumptionLandsOverMainsStamp`, `TestRecoverFromShipError_AnInboxStampOnMainIsResolvedAndPublishesItsPaths` and `TestAssembleFailureDigest_AFleetRebaseConflictIsItsOwnClassNotUnknown`, `TestAssembleFailureDigest_AGuardAbortOutranksAFleetRebaseConflict`, `TestShipJourney_AFailedContinueAfterAResolveKeepsTheAuditedCommit`, `TestShipJourney_AFailedStageReadIsNotTheLaneDeletingTheItem`, `TestShipJourney_ADeletionWithNoConsumedCopyStaysAConflict`, `TestShipJourney_AMergedItemThatLosesItsIdentityStaysAConflict`, `TestShipJourney_AnItemEditedByTwoLaneCommitsIsStampedOnce`, `TestFleetRebase_ALostAuditedCodeAfterAResolveRestoresTheLane`, `TestShipJourney_ARealFleetRebaseConflictCycleIsClassedAsAConflictNotUnknown`, `TestShipJourney_ADebuggerThatEndsAFleetRebaseConflictIsClassedAsAConflict`, `TestRecoverFromShipError_APeerThatLandedTheSameFileDoesNotEndACorrectLaneAsLostCode`, `TestRebaseRecordingConflicts_APeerThatLandedTheSameFileIsACorrectRebase`, `TestFleetRebase_DriftNamesTheCodeThatTheRebaseLost`, `TestReauditCycle_AnInboxStampOnMainIsResolvedAndPublishesItsPaths`, `TestResumeFleetRebaseAfterDebugger_AnInboxStampOnTheReentryRebasePublishesItsPaths`, `TestRebaseRecordingConflicts_AnItemAddedByTheLaneAndByMainMergesWithNoBaseStage` and `TestRecoveryReasonCode`.
- **The post-build normalizer** (`normalizeDerivedProjections`, `phase_bindings.go`). It runs only in the build iteration of `recordAndBranch`, before the audit binds the tree.
  - It checks each catalog entry whose inputs the cycle changed. It regenerates the entry when the check fails, and stages the outputs of the entry.
  - The audit iteration then binds the staged outputs with `worktreeContentSHA`. Thus the committed tree is the tree that the audit bound.
  - A failure only gives a WARN. The docs and skills gates stay the backstop.
- **Superseded-branch prune** (`prune_superseded_orphans.go`).
  - `PruneSupersededOrphans` keeps a superseded `cycle-*` ref if checked out (`KeptCheckedOut`) or bound in the continuation registry (`KeptBound`).
  - A refused `git branch -D` records `KeptDeleteFailed` and continues; errors loading worktrees or the registry abort before deleting any ref.
  - Pinned by `TestPruneSupersededOrphans_*` and `TestBranchesPrune*`.
- **Ship is a pure executor; recovery is the orchestrator's job.**
  - Ship returns a `ShipError`, and `recoverFromShipError` (`ship_recovery.go`) records it and asks `Recover()` for the next phase.
  - Recovery runs through `StaticPreset.Recover` regardless of dynamic routing stage; it handles errors rather than routing.
  - It aborts when recovery depth ends, `Recover()` routes to end, or the `ship→X` edge is illegal.
- **Composition carry-forward** (`composition_carryforward.go`, `composition_scoped_review.go`).
  - Core owns RUNG 0 and RUNG 2; `cmd/evolve` injects snapshot, gate, verdict writer and scoped reviewer closures through `Option`.
  - `CompositionFastPathWired` and `ScopedMergeReviewWired` prove wiring (`cmd/evolve/cmd_cycle_composition_test.go`), and `IntersectingHunks` builds payloads.
  - `identityCarryForward` (`identity_carry_forward.go`, ADR-0105 B3) verifies identical rebases with `treedelta`, runs composed gates, and records `identical-rebase` to the root ledger.
  - Every rung writes through `<projectRoot>/.evolve/ledger.jsonl` and its evidence store rather than the cycle worktree ([incident](../../incidents/2026-10-06-carry-refused-ledger-verify-f5.md)).
  - Pinned by `TestCarryRecord_VerifiesAfterItsCycleWorktreeIsRemoved` and `TestCompositionCarry_ChainsFromTheProjectLedgersTipThroughALinkedWorktree` through `identity_carry_export_test.go`.
  - `carriedAudit` picks the auditor row and requires its base to match the authored base (`TestRouteRebasedExplanation_AByteIdenticalRebaseShipsOnTheCarriedVerdict`, `TestRouteRebasedExplanation_AMainThatMovedBeforeTheAuditStillCarries`, `TestRouteRebasedExplanation_ACarryThatCannotBeProvenReturnsToAudit`, `TestCarriedAudit_ARowNamingNoBaseNeverCarriesEvenOnAnEmptyBase`).
  - All `MissingComposedGates` sites decline through `declineComposedGates`, emitting `ORCHESTRATOR_COMPOSED_GATE_DECLINED` with bounded `tail_<gate>` fields (`composed_gate_decline_test.go`, `composed_gate_tail_bound_test.go`).
  - Since 2026-10-21, RUNG 0 is retired (see the ship recovery bullets). Thus only RUNG 2 and the identity carry remain as trip sites of `MissingComposedGates`.
  - Ship re-proves only `identical-rebase` records (`carry.go`). Thus a RUNG 2 `scoped-review` record does not carry at ship. RUNG 2 has no production reviewer, and stage 7 of the landing-queue plan retires it.
- **A moved main over an unchanged lane** (`head_moved_rebind.go`, since 2026-10-27). In wave 145, cycles 2058, 2060, 2062 and 2063 each have three or more auditor rows in `.evolve/ledger.jsonl` (counted on 2026-10-28). Each ship found `AUDIT_BINDING_HEAD_MOVED` or `GIT_FLEET_REBASE_NEEDED`, and the router sent the first code to Audit with no rebase.
  - **Capacity kill** (`ship_capacity.go`). A gate that hits its deadline on an unchanged lane tree gives `REPO_CONTRACT_CAPACITY`. The first kill routes to Ship with no re-audit. A second capacity kill right after a capacity recovery (`cs.ShipRecoveryCode` holds the code) parks the lane: `parkOnCapacity` emits `GATE_REPO_CAPACITY_PARKED` and the recovery aborts. Another recovery in between resets the check, because the tree changed. Pinned by `TestRecovery_ADeadlineKillOnAnUnchangedTreeRetriesWithoutReaudit`, `TestRecovery_ASecondKillParksTheLaneWithACapacityCode`, `TestRecovery_AnotherFailureAfterACapacityKillKeepsItsRoute` and `TestRecovery_ACapacityKillAfterAnotherRecoveryRetriesAgain`.
  - `cycleRun.carryAuditedChangeOverMovedMain` runs before `recoverFromShipError`. For `AUDIT_BINDING_HEAD_MOVED`, `commitPendingAuditedChange` commits the pending change as an `Evolve-Carrier` commit on its base. `GIT_FLEET_REBASE_NEEDED` reads the same query. The error then becomes `GIT_FLEET_REBASE_NEEDED` with `shiperr.OriginalCodeKey`. `asFleetRebase` makes this change for this path and for `staleBaseAsRebase`.
    - One query, `pendingAuditedChange`, reads the conditions. The cycle has its own worktree, and HEAD is the cycle base and the audited base. The audited tree differs from the base tree. Then the `treefence` tree of the worktree must be the audited tree.
    - A lane that the audit judged unchanged is not pending. So a stray untracked file does not buy an audit round.
  - `carrier_commit.go` holds the carrier contract: `carrierLabel` (`cycle-<n>/<run-id>`), the trailer key `Evolve-Carrier` and `commitCarrier`. The unwind, the debugger carrier, this path and the resume heal (`carrierAtHead`) all use it. Thus a writer and the reader cannot drift.
  - The recovery can end with no route, for example when the budget is spent. Then `pendUnusedCarrier` pends the carrier at HEAD on its parent.
  - The fleet-rebase recovery then runs the ADR-0105 ladder: the unwind, the rebase, the pend and the identity carry. The identity carry runs the composed-tree gates on the merged tree and writes the `identical-rebase` record. Ship re-binds the audit to the new `main` through that record, so no audit runs and `AuditDispatches` does not change.
  - A changed lane tree, a lane that is not pending on its audited base, or an in-place cycle keeps the old route to Audit. The reason goes to stderr.
  - `ORCHESTRATOR_SHIP_RECOVERY_PATH` records the path for each code that names a moved `main`. `fields.path` is `rebind`, `reaudit`, `rebuild`, `debugger`, `end`, or the name of the next phase for any other route. `fields.original_code` is the code that ship raised, and `fields.reason` tells why a moved HEAD was audited again.
  - The cycle-spend reader (`cyclespend.Read`) counts `AuditDispatches`, so a re-bind is not an audit round.
  - Limit: a carry chains from the authored base only (ADR-0105 B3). After one carry, a second move of `main` returns to Audit.
  - Pinned by `TestRunCycleFromPhase_AMovedHeadOverAnUnchangedLaneRebindsWithoutAnAuditRound`, `TestRunCycleFromPhase_AMovedHeadOverAChangedLaneStillGetsAnAuditRound` and the `TestCarryAuditedChangeOverMovedMain_*`, `TestCommitPendingAuditedChange_*`, `TestRecoverShipError_AnExhaustedBudgetPendsTheCarrierBackOnItsBase` and `TestPendUnusedCarrier_*` tests.
- **Carryover seam** (`carryover_lifecycle.go`, [03-carryover-lifecycle.md](../decomposition/03-carryover-lifecycle.md)). An `Orchestrator` built as a literal gets its lifecycle lazily. `wiredCarryover` reads the Signal Center live, because tests apply `WithSignalCenter` after construction. The exported package-level facades run on a Null-Object lifecycle for tests and ACS predicates and carry `Deprecated:` notes naming the wired path.
- **Console lease** (`console_lease.go`, [ADR-0080](../adr/0080-runtime-console-plane-separation.md) S4). It is an exact-path, expiring operator waiver for the tree-diff guard. It is read from the git common dir and adopted once at cycle start; the cycle log records its digest.
- **Continuation-on-fail** ([ADR-0076](../adr/0076-convergence-architecture.md) C). At the preserve decision, the dirty worktree of a FAILed cycle gets a snapshot commit on its cycle branch. The workspace also gets a manifest. Both happen only when the carry-forward screen reads the snapshot Clean against main.
  - **Binding.** The manifest is also registered under the todo ids of the lane pin, the host record first. So a wave-planner lane with no inbox claim still leaves a binding. Adoption resolves claims first and lane scope second.
  - **Adoption.** The adopting cycle provisions from the snapshot commit, so work comes through git history and never as dirty state. It keeps the original base as its review base, so the whole work gets a review. It serves the prior findings to Build. `advanceContinuationBase` then merges the pinned main tip while the merge is clean.
  - **Retention (design A2).** `prepareAdoptedTree` moves two kinds of artifact of the ancestor to the dated `docs/private/research/archived-*/` archive. These are its unpublished explanation records and its predicate package (`go/acs/cycle<M>`, absent at the base).
  - **Why.** Before 2026-09-30 the predicate package stayed in the tree, so the added-test self-check of the build floor ran the contract of the ancestor. Cycle 1764 failed on the allow-list fence of 1761, which counted each file that main gained after the base of 1761.
  - **Failure.** An invalid continuation falls back to fresh provisioning, loudly. A declined binding is released at the decline site ([ADR-0085](../adr/0085-continuation-registry-release.md)).
- **A retracting phase runs alone** ([ADR-0149](../adr/0149-a-test-writing-phase-that-states-fail-on-its-own-work-is-retracted-to-its-dispatch-tree.md) D6). `batchArchetype` gives a `retract_on_fail` phase no archetype, so `evaluateBatch` never runs it beside a read-only phase. The fence of that phase can restore a retracted test. Pinned by `TestEvaluateBatchAt_ARetractOnFailPhaseNeverRunsBesideAnotherPhase`.
- **The repair directive of a continuation** ([failure-rate review §4.4](../../incidents/2026-08-17-failure-rate-review-1481-1503.md)). After the archive, `continuationRepairFirst` asks `continuation.RepairFirst` for the reds of the prior try that are still in the tree. The directive goes under `CtxKeyContinuationRepairFirst`, and Build renders it first. An unreadable verdict is a WARN, and the descriptive findings still go to Build. Pinned by `TestRunCycle_AContinuationWithAnInheritedRedLeadsWithTheRepairDirective` and `TestRunCycle_AContinuationWithoutARedCarriesNoRepairDirective`.
- **Two signals for the optimistic admission record** ([ADR-0143](../adr/0143-admission-holds-only-shared-edits-and-the-ship-gate-owns-api-drift.md) W1b). `ORCHESTRATOR_COMPOSED_GATE_DECLINED` carries `fields.gates`, the failed composed-tree gates. A fleet rebase conflict in `recoverFromShipError` publishes `ORCHESTRATOR_FLEET_REBASE_CONFLICT` (a `ship.warning` with `fields.paths`). It is not a landing-conflict ship error, so the oversight does not count the conflict twice. Pinned by `TestRecoverFromShipError_AFleetRebaseConflictPublishesItsPaths` and the decline tests.
- **`ChangedWorktreePaths`** is the exported projection of the host reviewer's changed-path derivation, so `evolve phase verify build` cannot drift from it ([ADR-0077](../adr/0077-docs-floor-for-architecture-changes.md)).
- **`WorktreeEvolveInvocation`** gives the one invocation that runs a worktree's own `evolve` from its source: `go run ./cmd/evolve <args>` in `<worktree>/go`, with `EVOLVE_WORKTREE_ROOT=<worktree>` after the inherited environment. `regenerateDerivedArtifact` and the audit skills-drift gate use it, so a lane's generator change is regenerated and graded by the lane's own code. Pinned by `TestWorktreeEvolveInvocation_RunsTheWorktreesOwnEvolveFromItsSource`.
- **`ChangedWorktreePathsSinceBase`** lists the paths changed since the cycle base, committed work included, plus untracked files. Without a base it diffs HEAD. The build-floor reviewer (`changedFloorPaths`) and the audit skills-drift gate use it. Pinned by `TestChangedWorktreePathsSinceBase_SeesCommittedWorkFromTheBase`.
- **The debugger's signal-branch gate is keyed on `successorStrategy`, not the phase name** (ADR-0058). The debugger is a control phase with no registry home, so `builtinControlSpec` supplies its default `branching_strategy: signal`, overlaid by `specFor` with registry precedence. A registry `debugger` entry overriding to `verdict` branching skips the entire signal branch, so no successor is scheduled for any decision signal, including `BLOCK`.
- **`successorStrategy` gates the retro history branch like the debugger's signal branch: config-driven, never name-keyed.**
  - `recordAndBranch` and resume take the history branch only when `branching_strategy` resolves to `history`.
  - Unset catalogs or omitted fields keep the default (retro to history; other phases to verdict).
  - Overriding to verdict branching skips the branch on both live and resume (`RetroDecision` stays empty, no successor scheduled).
  - Pinned by `TestSuccessorStrategy`, `TestRecordAndBranch_RetroGateIsStrategyKeyed`, `TestRecordAndBranch_RetroDegradesToHistoryWhenUnconfigured`, `TestResume_RetroDegradesToHistoryWhenUnconfigured` and `TestResume_RetroGateIsStrategyKeyed`.
- **Persisted shapes are additive-only** (`bytestability_test.go`). Legacy JSON keys for `State`, `LedgerEntry` and `CycleState` stay unchanged; new fields use `omitempty` so old files keep their bytes.
- **`RealInboxForTest`** holds the real inboxmover functions. `continuation_adopt_inbox_test.go` (package `core_test`) injects them from `init`, because an in-package inboxmover import is a cycle while `core_test → inboxmover → adapters/ledger → core` is not.
- **Contract-block CLI escalation** (`contract_escalation.go`).
  - Re-dispatching one CLI after a contract block can burn corrections and demote the gate to advisory.
  - Escalation retries the re-dispatch on another CLI family by promoting the target through `llmroute.ApplySoftOverlay`.
  - The overlay reverts when the ladder ends and never edits the profile on disk.
  - It triggers at the second consecutive block (`ReviewResult.Blocks`) or second identical remediation rejection (`ReviewResult.Remediation`).
  - Zero-block rejections without remediation never escalate.
- **Escalation picks from the router's decision** (2026-10-06, L1b of the CLI routing table, [ADR-0119](../adr/0119-one-routing-table-one-resolver.md)). `routingDecision` resolves the agent and the phase through the CLI router of the composition root (`WithCLIRouter`). `CLIRouterWired` reports that router, and `TestOrchestrator_CLIRouterWiredReportsTheInjectedRouter` pins it. Thus `contractDispatchCLI` and `contractEscalationTarget` read the chain that the runner walks, with the probe, the bench and the universal tail. The first candidate of another family that `Decision.Allows` accepts wins.
- **Escalation compares no drivers in core** (I-17, X19a). `llmroute.EscalationTarget` does the family comparison, and core passes the result on as an opaque `llmroute.Binding`. The universal claude fallback goes only into a legacy decision, so a declared table is never left.
- **Escalation without a router.** A one-profile legacy router over the profile of the phase (`cliroute.SingleProfile`) keeps the old behavior. A routing refusal is a stderr line and no escalation, never a crash. `TestContractEscalation_PicksFromTheTable` and `TestContractEscalation_NeverLeavesTheAllowedSet` pin the behavior. Before L1b, escalation called `llmroute.Resolve` and checked each candidate with `policy.ValidatePin`.
- **Only an identical contract block escalates.**
  - Two blocks match when their `deliverable.Violation.Code` sets intersect (subset, superset or equal).
  - Whole-string compares miss partially repaired defect sets where an incapable CLI repeats the same defect class.
  - When a block yields no codes, identity falls back to `normalizeReasonForFingerprint`; zero prior blocks match so cross-ladder aborts still escalate.
- **A phase whose whole dispatch chain is one CLI family gets a structured re-prompt instead of nothing.** `composeContractSalvageRetry` re-dispatches the same CLI, in the same round, with the same budget. Only the directive changes. It holds the output of the contract validator verbatim under its own heading. The retry adds no dispatch and no correction, and it does not change `Driver`. It is disjoint from escalation, so one block never spends its budget twice.
- **`contractArtifactDetermined` is an allowlist, fail-closed classifier**, not a denylist. An unseen violation code returns false (skip hash short-circuit). It has no production caller yet, prepared for a future hash-equality short-circuit without building scope creep alongside it.
- **A contract-gate demotion stages an autofile intent through `dispositionrouter` rather than writing `.evolve/inbox` directly.**
  - Mid-flight inbox writes race `inboxmover.Claim` renames across fleet lanes; `recurrence.ApplyBoundary` is the only sanctioned inbox writer.
  - `ContractGateDemotion{Phase, CLI, Cycle, Blocks, Weight, Reason}.Intent()` builds the `contract-gate-demoted-<phase>` intent with `priority_class: correctness` ([ADR-0121](../adr/0121-inbox-priority-is-a-computed-rank.md)).
  - Exported so `cmd/evolve`'s `TestInboxWriters_EveryAutofiledItemCarriesAKnownClass` stages the real intent through `recurrence.ApplyBoundary`.
  - Its `Pattern` (`contract gate demoted enforce->advisory on phase <phase> (cli=<cli>)`) is both recurrence identity and title stem (`TestContractGateDemotion_IntentIsACorrectnessClassedConsoleAutofile`).
- **Raw tool output is kept only on fail** (`pruneToolOutputOnPass`, `tool_output_retention.go`, called by `completeCycle` after the dossier). At the seal of a PASS cycle it deletes the files that `gcpolicy.ToolOutputFiles` names (`ship-repocontract-scan.log`, `integration-tier.log`) from the run dir. Any other verdict keeps them for forensics. A delete failure is a WARN, and the cycle stays sealed.
- **The closeout writes the shadow retrospectives** (`writeShadowRetrospectives`, `retrospective_shadow.go`, called by `completeCycle` after the dossier). It passes the project root, the workspace, the cycle and the carryover todos of the state to `recoveryretro.WriteShadow`. At stage `shadow` that writes only into `<workspace>/retrospectives/`. An error is one WARN line, and the cycle stays sealed. Spec: [recovery-retrospective.md](../recovery-retrospective.md) §5. Pinned by `TestRunCycle_TheCloseoutWritesTheShadowRetrospectiveIntoTheWorkspace` and `TestWriteShadowRetrospectives_AFailureIsAWarnLineOnly`.
  - `archivePollutedWorkspace` names its archive with `gcpolicy.PollutedArchiveName`, so gc discovery takes the archive as a dead run.
  - Pinned by `TestCompleteCycle_APassSealDeletesTheRawToolOutputAndKeepsTheRest`, `TestCompleteCycle_ASealThatIsNotPassKeepsTheRawToolOutput` and `TestArchivePollutedWorkspace_MintsANameThatGCDiscoveryAccepts`. The plan is [logging-and-process-hygiene-2026-10.md](../../plans/logging-and-process-hygiene-2026-10.md).
- **Chronicle digest seeding** (`seedChronicleDigest`, called once per cycle from `planCycle`, with `WithChronicleConfig` defaulting to `shadow`).
  - `off` writes and injects nothing; `shadow` writes `recent-outcomes.md` from dossier history and failure state without injecting `Context["recent_outcomes"]`.
  - `enforce` also injects the rendered bytes into each phase request for scout and triage. Digest failures WARN and proceed.
- **Fleet scope resolution** (`planCycle`). The host value of the fleet scope in the env snapshot wins over `lane-scope.json`, because a cell can rewrite that file ([ADR-0160](../adr/0160-the-host-lineage-record-is-the-one-trust-root-for-what-a-cycle-may-do-to-the-queue.md)). The file is read only when no host value exists. An env-scoped run pins its own file before any phase runs. `fleet_scope_paths` then gives the live inbox record path of each scoped id beside the id list. Only PENDING ids resolve, so the list omits a consumed or non-inbox id and does not point at stale evidence.
- **`finalizeCycle`'s floor stack** runs in order after `finalizeOutcome`:
  - cross-artifact invariants (four advisory verifiers over cycle artifacts, recorded beside the coherence floor without affecting verdicts);
  - verdict-coherence floor (negative verdict with green artifacts halts as a forged verdict unless `Verify` self-heals a clean-exit late write to PASS);
  - lost-landing floor (PASS verdict on a cycle whose ship artifacts show contention without landing);
  - silent-no-ship warning (phases ran, ship never landed, no audit-advisory block recorded, worktree changes discarded).
- **Graduated remediation** (`maybeRemediate`, between `reviewAndGuard` and `recordAndBranch`).
  - On a FAIL from a remediable deterministic gate with rounds left, the builder runs once with the gate report as directive.
  - The same gate runs again and its fresh verdict replaces the original so downstream phases run unchanged.
  - `workflow.remediation_rounds` caps rounds (default zero) and `workflow.remediable_phases` selects eligible phases; `remediationDenied` blocks judgment and control phases.
  - `CycleResult.Remediations` records every round so remediated cycles stay visible; mechanical fixes avoid discarding sound cycles.
- **Re-plan rejection telemetry** (`recordPlanRejectionsKind`, `decision_branch.go`).
  - `ValidatePlan` runs on raw re-plans before the floor clamp so unknown phases still leave attributable records.
  - Each plan kind (`plan` and `replan-<n>`) writes `advisor-rejections[-<kind>].json` without overwriting earlier records.
  - Pinned by `TestReplan_UnknownPhase_RecordsRejection`, `TestReplan_KnownPhasesOnly_NoSpuriousRejection` and `TestReplan_MultipleReplans_AllRejectionsRecorded`.
- **Post-phase leak classification has two guard-only exemptions plus the console lease** (`cyclerun_postreview.go`, `cycleRun.leakExemptions`). `filterRealLeaks` exempts specific legitimate main-tree writes so the guard stays armed for real escapes:
  - `isLegitimateMainTreePath`: a phase's own `.evolve/` workspace writes and orchestrator gate state;
  - a keyed path held by a live sibling lane or registered mint (`mainTreeOwnership.heldBySibling`); registry read errors disable the mint rule and quarantine through `activeVerifiedMints`.
  - Scout has no exemption: workspace is its eval home, main-tree copies relocate, and unowned evals charge as leaks.
  - Source files and both sides of leak-disguising renames classify as real leaks.
  - Pinned by `TestGuardIgnoresScoutEvalMaterialization`, `TestGuardStillChargesAnEvalNoLiveSiblingHolds`, `TestRunCycle_SiblingEvalWrittenDuringTDDIsNeitherChargedNorClaimed`, `TestMainTreeOwnership_HeldBySiblingNeedsALiveHolder`, `TestIsActiveMintPhasePath`, `TestVerifiedActiveMints`, `TestGuardRecoversCatalogWritesSourcePhaseLeak` and `TestGuardStillAbortsNonSourcePhaseLeak`.
- **`selectNext`'s spine-integrity re-check** (`cyclerun_select.go`).
  - After dynamic routing picks `next`, an unsatisfied spine re-digests once before deciding fail-open or fail-closed.
  - Aborts key on `cfg.SpineFloor` rather than `PhaseRecovery`, failing closed only on clean absence at `SpineFloor==enforce`.
  - Degraded reads, digest errors, or lower stages fail open with a WARN and `SpineFailOpen`; operators waive anchors through `cfg.Mandatory`.
- **The linear transition spine is a data table (`spineOrder`), walked by `spineNext`, instead of a per-phase switch in `Next`.** `spineOrder` is a config-independent trust anchor like the legality graph: config selects among legal edges (`on_pass`/`on_fail`), it cannot move the spine itself. `core.SpineFailOpen` is a re-export of the `cyclestate` leaf's own type, never a parallel struct, so core's records and the dossier's projection cannot drift apart. Pinned by `TestSpineNext_WalksCanonicalSpine`, `TestSpineNext_InjectedConfigSpine`, `TestSpineFailOpenAlias_IsTheCyclestateRecord` and the full byte-identity oracle `TestTransitionKernelOracle_Next`.
- **`UnsatisfiedSpineAnchor` is the exact complement of `SpineSatisfiedUpTo` and names the FIRST unsatisfied predecessor anchor.**
  - Fail-open telemetry points at the real cause instead of the last anchor or an invented missing anchor.
  - `recordSpineFailOpen` appends one `SpineFailOpen{Phase, MissingArtifact, Reason}` per fail-open event onto `CycleResult.SpineFailOpens` without collapsing repeats.
  - Pinned by `TestUnsatisfiedSpineAnchor_NamesTheMissingPredecessor`, `TestUnsatisfiedSpineAnchor_AgreesWithSpineSatisfiedUpTo`, `TestRecordSpineFailOpen_CarriesPhaseArtifactAndReason` and `TestRecordSpineFailOpen_UnrecordedCycleHasNoFailOpens`.
- **A retro PASS is graded as the cycle's FAIL for floor and dossier purposes** (`decideAfterRetro`).
  - Retro runs only after audit FAIL and is read-only outside its artifacts, so ship's tree stays identical to the rejected one.
  - Substituting FAIL preserves genuine FAIL/WARN paths and stops clean retrospectives from looking coherent to the verdict-incoherence floor.
  - See [the retry architecture review](../retry-architecture-review-2026-08-27.md).
  - Pinned by `TestDecideAfterRetro_RetroPASSIsNotRecovery`, `TestDecideAfterRetro_RetroPASSStillFacesTheFloor`, `TestDecideAfterRetro_RetroPASSReachesTheSameLadderAsFAIL`, `TestDecideAfterRetro_RetroFAILPathUnchanged` and `TestDecideAfterRetroRouted_RetroPASSCannotDropTheFloorSignal`.
- **`floorFailReason` is a forensic-only workspace artifact; the ADR-0072 coherence floor never reads it.**
  - Workspaces are agent-writable, so only `CycleState.AuditFailReasons` and `ShipFailReasons` in orchestrator memory (`recordFloorVerdictFailure`) mark a FAIL as diagnosed.
  - `persistFloorFailReasons` updates both carriers together and clears prior explanations when no error diagnostics remain; warning diagnostics clear both carriers.
  - `resetFloorFailReason` runs on every floor-phase dispatch, deferring when another phase owns the shared audit-fail-reason file.
  - Pinned by `TestDetectVerdictIncoherence_WorkspaceReasonFileAlone_StillHalts`, `TestDetectVerdictIncoherence_WarningOnlyDiags_StillHalts`, `TestRecordFloorVerdictFailure_PersistsAuditFailReason` and `TestPersistFloorFailReasons_ClobberAndReset`.
- **`detectVerdictIncoherence` is the ADR-0072 deterministic floor for verdict incoherence.**
  - A recorded FAIL or WARN whose on-disk audit and ACS artifacts are green halts as forged regardless of `strict_audit`.
  - RED artifacts stay genuine task failures, and `CycleOutcomeSkippedUnknown` stays with the orchestrator's judgment layer.
  - Diagnosed downgrades (`hasSubstantiveFailReasons` reading `AuditFailReasons` or `ShipFailReasons`) never halt.
  - Clean-exit late writes reconcile to PASS only when the on-disk report passes the injected `ContractVerifier` `Verify` chain (`WithContractVerifier`).
  - Malformed or nil-verifier reports stay unhealed and halt.

  - `floorClaimRefutation` overrules prose `verdict-incoherence` claims when persisted substantive fail reasons exist.

- **The disposition cross-check reads a harness-held digest record (2026-10-21).** `AssembleFailureDigest` writes the digest to the workspace and to `.evolve/failure-digests/cycle-<N>.json` (`failureDigestRecordPath`, `writeFailureDigestRecord`). No profile grants that directory.

The fields are `class_fingerprint`, `class_layer`, `class_recurrence`, `class_recorded_at`, `class_route` and `class_route_reason`. The route comes from `dispositionrouter.Decide`.   - `applyFailureDecisionFloor` and granted bookkeeping regrades run before `o.strategy.Decide`, so routed retries cannot override them.

- **`recoveryTarget` resolves a control phase's recovery successor from its `RecoveryMap`** (`spec.Recovery.Targets[key]`). It falls back to a literal default when the key, spec or catalog is absent, gated by `CanTransition`. Pinned by `TestRecoveryTarget_ConfigDriven` and `TestRecoveryTarget_DegradesWithoutCatalog`.
- **Every routing/plan decision artifact is hash-bound into the ledger the same way.**
  - Each step marshals, writes, hashes and appends a `LedgerEntry` with `ArtifactPath` and `ArtifactSHA256`; failures WARN and continue.
  - `recordPhasePlanKind` also binds `advisor-{prompt,response}-<kind>.txt` when present so mutated captures fail hash checks.
  - `recordPlanRejectionsKind` keys `advisor-rejections[-<kind>].json` by plan kind so multiple plans do not clobber earlier records.
  - `recordDebuggerDecision` binds `debug-decision.json` SHA; `recordRoutingDecision` appends `phase_skipped` rows for PSMAS-declined phases.

  - `advisorPlanInput` sets `RouteInput.LaneItems` from `LaneScopeIDs` and each id's live inbox record (id, kind, acceptance, deliverable kind).
  - `router.NormalizeDeliverableKind` drops unknown kind words; undeclared items inherit `.evolve/domain.json` through `resolveDeliverableKind`.

- **`enforceNext`** (`routing_dispatch.go`) maps the router's proposed phase to `core.Phase`. It returns the proposal only when it differs from the static successor and passes `CanTransition` and `SpineSatisfiedUpTo`; else the static successor stands.

A phase agent cannot write that directory. `loadResumeBootstrap` calls `applyResumeWitness` with the host cycle. A missing witness, a corrupt one, or one that names another cycle or `run_id` clears both values.

- **The sentinel-error taxonomy is closed, and "infra teardown" is a narrow union.**
  - `IsInfraTeardownError` matches only `ErrArtifactTimeout` (exit 81) and `ErrTransientBridgeFailure` (exit 80/85/86/124 or caller-cancelled -1).

- **The post-build checking phases run concurrently as one batch, then merge serially.**
  - `evaluateBatch` finds contiguous `evaluate` phases after build and before audit (skipping pre-build phases and batches under 2).
  - `dispatchEvaluateBatch` builds `PhaseRequest` serially, runs runners concurrently (`cfg.ParallelEvaluateConcurrency`), then merges outcomes serially.
  - Batch verdicts are weakest-link (`mergeVerdict`); hard dispatch errors record all outcomes before aborting.
  - `dispatchRunnerWithRetry` uses `evaluateBatchRetryOpts` (no ship recovery or backfill); `evaluateBatchAt` enters only at the first member.
  - Batched phases skip the correction ladder and tree-diff leak guard (read-only v1 gap, dormant `StageOff` default).
  - Pinned by `TestMergeVerdict`, `TestDispatchEvaluateBatch_Concurrent`, `TestDispatchEvaluateBatch_WeakestLink` and `TestDispatchEvaluateBatch_CrashSafety`.

  - Catalog-optional phases in `cfg.Mandatory`, in the ship floor, missing from catalog, or failing non-infra never skip.

- **A tdd predicate that reads a protected path stops the cycle as a FAIL with a route code.** The `PhaseTDD` branch of `selectNext` calls `routeProtectedPredicates`. The scan is in `internal/acspathscan`, and `WithProtectedSurface` injects the membership function. The termination is `tdd-protected-predicate-route`, the verdict is FAIL, and the item routes at the closeout. The design is in [the cycleoutcome note](internal-cycleoutcome.md#protected-predicate-route-tdd).

- **`CanTerminateEarly` is config-driven while `shipPlanned` stays a Go invariant** (ADR-0060).

- **The cycle-start operator-directives hook is called once per cycle.** An injected provider stamps its version into the ledger and threads `OperatorDirectives` to phases; empty sets leave dispatch unchanged (`TestOrchestrator_WithDirectivesProvider_SnapshotStampThread`, `TestOrchestrator_WithDirectivesProvider_EmptySetNoStamp`).
- **A repeat failure pattern never emits a bare `proceed`.** `escalateRetroReason` upgrades `proceed:` to `adapt:` once recurrence reaches 2+, exempting generic reset/loop-fatal patterns (`TestDecideAfterRetro_NthOccurrenceForcesAdapt`, `TestC662_EscalateRetroReasonIgnoresGenericPatterns`).
- **One classification of a walled dispatch** (`quota_exhaustion.go`, [ADR-0106](../adr/0106-logic-first-delivery.md) Q1).
  - `isQuotaWall` reads exit 85 after a runner exhausts its family chain (`bridgechain.WallKeeper`).
  - Correction, remediation, and resumed review paths all pause through `pauseForQuota` with `DEFERRED`, `all_families_exhausted`, and `quota.paused`.
  - Pinned by `TestRunCycle_AllFamilies85_CheckpointsAndDefers`, `TestRunCycle_WalledRemediationRerun_DefersLikeAFirstDispatch`, `TestRunCycleFromPhase_WalledResumedCorrection_Defers`, `TestRunCycleFromPhase_AWalledResumedDispatchNamesItsWalk` and `TestErrAllFamiliesExhausted_ClaimsOnlyThatTheDispatchEndedAtAWall`.
- **The quota-wall rule** (`isQuotaWall`, `ErrAllFamiliesExhausted`, since 2026-10-09). One dispatch is walled when the runner returns exit 85. The runner walks its whole chain inside one `Run`, so one sample is enough. `allFamiliesQuotaExhausted` keeps its two-sample rule until its tests model the tiered chain. The runner returns 85 only when the walk has an exit 85, and each other started rung is proven exhausted or neutral ([internal-phases-runner](internal-phases-runner.md)). Thus a lost pane or a stall with `healthy` or `unknown` usage is never a quota pause (cycle 1853, [incident](../../incidents/cycle-1853-secondary-defects.md)).
- **The pause carries its wall.** `core.WalkError` holds a `Wall`, one `FamilyEnd` row for each family that the walk met (CLI, pattern, reset time, evidence). `pauseForQuota` copies `Wall.Ends` to `CycleState.QuotaWall` and to `CycleResult.QuotaWall` before the checkpoint hook runs. The checkpoint reads the reset evidence of those rows only. A resumed pause merges the walls of all tries, so a login at the first try stays on the wall (`TestRetryPhaseRunner_AResumedPauseCarriesTheMergedWall`). Pinned also by `TestRunCycle_AWalledPauseHandsTheWalkedCLIsToTheCheckpoint` and `TestRunCycle_ALedgerFaultDoesNotLoseTheQuotaPause`.
- **The router quota wall stays in memory (`router_observer.go`, 2026-11-11).** When the router plan ends at a usage-limit wall, `planCycle` keeps the wall of the plan error in `cyclePlan.routerWall`. `pauseAtRouterWall` pauses the cycle from it before the first phase.
  - Before 2026-11-11 the wall went through the file `router-quota-wall.json` inside one process, and each file error was dropped. A failed save then skipped the pause. No file is left to fail.
  - A failed bench of a quota family joins the pause error, so the cycle still pauses as DEFERRED. A family that is only logged out is not benched as a quota wall.
  - Pinned by `TestRunCycle_ARouterPlanEndingAtAQuotaWallPausesTheCycle` and `TestRunCycle_ABenchThatFailsAtTheRouterWallStillPausesAndNamesTheFamily`.
- **A login is not a quota pause.** `pauseForQuota` emits `quota.paused` only when `Wall.CountsAsQuotaPause` is true: the wall has a quota row, or no row has a recorded cause (a bare exit 85). A wall with only login rows, or only rows of another cause, emits no `quota.paused`, so `wave.Cycle.QuotaPauses` does not rise. The login signal `login.required` and the wave event `login-required` report such a wall. Pinned by `TestPauseForQuota_AWallWithNoQuotaRowFiresNoQuotaPaused`.
- **The abnormal epilogue** (`abnormalEpilogue`).
  - RunCycle defers it. It acts only when the cycle did not reach the normal closeout.
  - A quota pause has its own checkpoint, so it does nothing for it.
  - A cancelled cycle context (a lane lease deadline or an operator stop) keeps its phase for resume. Since 2026-10-27 the epilogue then runs only the deadline stamp (`deadlineStamp`). The salvage snapshot and the continuation stamp run on `context.WithoutCancel` with their own budget, `defaultDeadlineStampBudget` (5 s). The budget is half of `LaneLaunchWaitDelay` (10 s), the `WaitDelay` of the lane launch (`execCycleLaunch`), so the stamp cannot hold the lane past its SIGKILL. A log line `deadline stamp after the cycle context ended (<cause>)` records each try.
  - Why: before 2026-10-27 the epilogue returned on a cancelled context. The deadline stops of cycles 2008, 2010, 2013, 2015 and 2034 thus left no snapshot and lost 326.8 LLM minutes of work. Pinned by `TestAbnormalEpilogue_LaneDeadlineCancelStillSnapshotsAndStamps` and `TestAbnormalEpilogue_DeadlineStampStopsAtItsOwnBudget`.
  - It is best effort and loud, and each step tolerates a failure of the others.
  - The steps are the failure digest, the `closeout` skip, the FAIL seal (ADR-0101), the dossier, the continuation manifest (ADR-0076) and the `aborted` state.
  - The seal reason is the template plus `epilogueCauseSuffix(cause)`. It is `cause=` for a task error and `teardown=` for an infra teardown (ADR-0072), with the tail of `causeHead`.
  - Since 2026-10-09 a teardown with an artifact-timeout cause names that cause first (`teardown=pane_lost: …`). Thus a lost pane never shares a fingerprint with a `review_pause` timeout.
  - Pinned by `TestEpilogueCauseSuffix_APaneLossNeverMergesWithAReviewPauseTimeout` and `TestAbnormalEpilogue_ADossierOrStateWriteFaultStillLeavesTheDigestAndTheAbortedPhase`.

- **`emitPhaseBindings`** (`phase_bindings.go`) writes per-agent provenance ledger rows required for ship verification on both `RunCycle` and `RunCycleFromPhase`, avoiding `AUDIT_BINDING_HEAD_MOVED`.
- **The fork point** (`forkPoint`, `git merge-base HEAD main`) stays put as a lane's base when main moves after a rebase (`TestRouteRebasedExplanation_BindsTheForkPointWhenMainMovesOn`).
- **`normalizeScoutGoalHash` reconciles a scout↔triage lane-identity mismatch by machine-stamping the pin's goal hash into the scout report.** A transcription flip in the echoed goal hash can repeat on retry. The pin stays authoritative; stamps fire only on 64-hex hashes, failing open with WARN (`TestNormalizeScoutGoalHash_StampsMismatchToPin`, `TestNormalizeScoutGoalHash_NonCanonicalEchoRefused`, `TestNormalizeScoutGoalHash_FailOpen`, `TestNormalizeScoutGoalHash_WriteFailureIsLoudNoOp`).
- **Fleet mode skips the whole-cycle global project lock** (`EVOLVE_FLEET=1`). Each fleet cycle has its own process, worktree and workspace. Each shared resource has its own flock (`state.json`, the ledger chain, `.evolve/ship.lock`), so the coarse lock only makes fleet cycles refuse each other. `newCycleRun` and `RunCycleFromPhase` both call `acquireCycleLock`, so a resumed cycle uses the same rule, and the sequential loop (no `EVOLVE_FLEET`) keeps the lock. Before that, a resumed fleet lane took the lock, and the next resumed lane refused (wave 150, cycle 2107). Pinned by `TestRunCycle_FleetMode_SkipsGlobalLock`, `TestRunCycle_Default_AcquiresGlobalLock`, `TestRunCycleFromPhase_AFleetLaneResumesWhileAnotherLaneHoldsTheProjectLock` and `TestRunCycleFromPhase_OutsideAFleetTheResumeTakesTheProjectLock`.
- **The integrity floor activates the advisor's plan, not just Enforce mode** (ADR-0024 §1).
  - At `Stage>=Advisory`, the orchestrator builds a whole-cycle plan, clamps it (`ClampPlanToFloor`), and keeps build and audit mandatory.
  - Planner errors degrade to the configurable spine; under hybrid cadence (ADR-0024 §2), the per-transition Proposer runs only at branch transitions.
  - `Stage:Off` ignores scripted plans and runs the legacy spine (`TestFloorActivationCycle` in `floor_activation_scenarios_test.go`).
- **The infra-teardown union predicate is enforced single-sourced by an AST uniqueness scan.**
  - `IsInfraTeardownError` is the one allowed place for the timeout-or-transient union; `findInfraTeardownUnionSpellings` checks `internal/core`, `internal/bridge`, and `internal/phases/runner`.
  - Timeout-only sites (`adviseOnUnclassifiedFailure`, `isArtifactTimeout`, `wiredFailureDiag`, `DeliveryFailureCause`) must reference `ErrArtifactTimeout` alone, never transient errors or the union.
  - `optionalInfraSkip` must match `IsOptionalSkippableError` for every error shape.
  - Pinned by `TestInfraTeardownUnion_SpelledExactlyOnce`, `TestInfraTeardownUnion_ScanCoversConsumerPackages`, `TestInfraTeardownUnion_DetectsPlantedDuplicateOutsideCore`, `TestOptionalInfraSkip_GateAgreesWithIsOptionalSkippableError`, `TestIsTransientBridgeError_StaysTransientOnly`, `TestWritePhaseFailureDiag_TimeoutOnlyNotWidened` and `TestTimeoutOnlySites_NotWidenedToUnion`.
- **The correction ladder's re-dispatch is a typed interaction, recorded to a per-phase ledger and per-cycle rollup** (ADR-0045 I1).
  - Each re-dispatch appends an `interaction.Outcome` (`correction_redispatch`) to `<workspace>/<phase>-interactions.ndjson`, resolved as `accepted` or `rejected_again`.
  - `RunCycle`'s deferred persistence flushes `interaction-summary.json` on normal and abort exits (`TestCorrectionRedispatch_RecordsInteractionOutcome`, `TestCorrectionRedispatch_ExhaustionRecordsRejectedAgain`).
- **The scout-eval-materialization correction round-trip runs against the real `evalgate.materializationGate`.**
  - Remediation directives drop the "do not change unrelated files" clause so missing `.evolve/evals/<slug>.md` files can be created.
  - Non-complying re-dispatches keep failing; `materialization_correction_delivery_test.go` sits in `core_test` to break the `evalgate` import cycle.
  - Pinned by `TestMaterializationCorrectionDelivery_RemediationReachesReDispatchAndClearsTheRealGate` and `TestMaterializationCorrectionDelivery_NonComplyingReDispatchStaysRejected`.
- **RUNG 2** (`mergerung2.go`) reviews only hunks intersecting between the audited and rebased diffs, escalating to RUNG 3 on entanglement. Empty intersections pass without calling the injected reviewer; LLM resolutions require `ResolutionMatchesAudited` patch-id verification, and malformed diffs fail closed.
- **`isActiveMintPhasePath`'s exemption is clamp parity, not provenance.**
  - With OS sandbox off, `verifiedActiveMints` exempts only `.evolve/phases/<name>` and `phase.json` for registered, TTL-fresh, valid specs.
  - It `Lstat`s small regular files, checks `PhaseSpec` validity (`ValidateUserSpec`), and checks kebab-case `mintNameRE` before filesystem access.
  - Pinned by `TestIsActiveMintPhasePath` and `TestVerifiedActiveMints`.
- **A judgment phase's FAIL verdict teaches without halting** (`judgment_lesson.go`).
  - `premise-challenge`, `plan-review`, and `adversarial-review` return FAIL with `err == nil`, bypassing `recordFloorVerdictFailure` and `recordFailureLearning`.
  - `recordJudgmentLesson` writes carryover todos rather than `FailedRecord`, preventing objecting phases from halting batches.
  - Pinned by `TestRecordAndBranch_PremiseChallengeFAILTeaches`, `TestRecordAndBranch_PremiseChallengeFAILDoesNotRecordFailedApproach` and `TestJudgmentLessonFullPath_PremiseChallengeSentinelFAILTeachesWithoutHalting`.
- **The Task Contract reaches any phase that asks for it.** `seedTaskContract` seeds tdd, build, audit, and any catalog phase listing `task_contract` in `prompt_context` (such as `code-review`). Pinned by `TestSeedTaskContract_AUserPhaseThatAsksForItInPromptContextGetsTheBlock`.
- **The code-review phase's findings are recorded at the completion boundary** (`review_findings.go`, [ADR-0124](../adr/0124-code-review-phase.md)).
  - When a `code-review` dispatch completes with any verdict but FAIL, `recordReviewFindings` hands the workspace, the cycle and the round to `internal/codereview`. The round is `codereview.RoundFrom` over the persisted `CompletedPhases`, so the delta re-review is round 2 on resume too.
  - `internal/codereview` appends findings to `defect-ledger.json` and core emits `REVIEW_FINDINGS` stamped with run id and `codereview.Settings`.
  - Both `phaseCompletionRecord.persist` and `recordBatchMember` call it; FAIL and SKIPPED reviews record nothing.
- **An optional evaluate phase degrades on an exhausted correction ladder** (`correction_exhaustion.go`, [ADR-0124](../adr/0124-code-review-phase.md) fix round 1, H2).
  - `Orchestrator.skippableOptional` is the one optional-phase predicate: not configured mandatory (`isConfiguredMandatory`), a catalog spec that is `Optional`, and not in the ship floor. `optionalInfraSkip`, `postShipObserverSkip` and the exhaustion degrade all call it, so a phase the operator lists in `config.mandatory_phases` is never skipped by any of them.
  - `degradesOnExhaustion` matches optional evaluate phases past the ship floor; `degradesRejection` degrades present malformed deliverables while absent ones abort (`TestOptionalSkip_RealDeliverableGateFreshAndResume`).
  - `reviewInputFor` sets `ReviewInput.BreakerExempt` from `degradesOnExhaustion`, suppressing breaker-count escalation and salvage re-prompts.
  - `rejectAfterCorrections` (fresh, records failure learning) and `reviewResumedDeliverable` (resume, records none) degrade through `degradeExhaustedReview` to WARN, `contract_exhaustion_skip`, and SKIPPED.
  - `noteContractExhaustionSkip` appends the ledger row and emits `REVIEW_SKIPPED{reason: malformed}` with the round for `code-review`.
  - Pinned by `TestReviewWithCorrections_AnOptionalEvaluatePhaseThatExhaustsItsCorrectionsIsSkippedNotAborted`, `TestReviewWithCorrections_ABreakerExemptLadderNeverWarnsOfABreakerThatCannotOpen`, `TestReviewWithCorrections_AnOptionalEvaluatePhaseWhoseDeliverableIsAbsentStillAborts`, `TestReviewWithCorrections_AnotherOptionalEvaluatePhaseDegradesWithoutAReviewSignal`, `TestReviewWithCorrections_ADegradedReviewStillRecordsItsFailureLearning`, `TestReviewWithCorrections_ASkippedReviewNamesTheRoundItWouldHaveBeen` and `TestReviewWithCorrections_OneCorrectionIsCountedInTheAbortMessage`.
  - Pinned also by `TestReviewWithCorrections_AnAbortWithNoCorrectionsTeachesTheRetroItsFirstAttempt`, `TestReviewResumedDeliverable_AResumedReviewIsBreakerExemptAndDegradesLikeAFreshOne`, `TestReviewResumedDeliverable_StillAbortsOutsideTheDegrade`, `TestReviewWithCorrections_APhaseOutsideTheOptionalEvaluateClassStillAborts`, `TestCodeReviewCompletion_RecordsItsFindingsAsShadowRowsAndSignalsThem`, `TestCodeReviewCompletion_ASecondDispatchIsRoundTwo`, `TestCodeReviewCompletion_AFailedReviewOrAnotherPhaseRecordsNothing`, `TestDispatchEvaluateBatch_RecordsTheCodeReviewsFindingsLikeTheSerialPath` and `TestJudgmentLesson_WiredInLockstepWithFloorRecorder`.
- **`judgmentTeachingPhases` is an explicit set, never derived.**
  - The derivation (`remediationDenied[p] && !isAuthoritativePhase(p)`) sweeps in `PhaseRetro` and `PhaseDebugger`.
  - Retro writes lessons, so teaching from a retro FAIL recurses into its output. `recordFailureLearning` excludes `PhaseRetro` by a guard that a derived call skips.
  - Audit is absent because it is authoritative and `recordFloorVerdictFailure` already records it.
  - `plan-review` is listed without a catalog `phase.json` so the lesson works when it gets one.
  - Pinned by `TestJudgmentLesson_ControlPhasesDoNotTeach` and `TestJudgmentLessonEndToEnd_ControlPhaseFAILReachesNoPlannerPrompt`.
- **Token telemetry S4 projects the terminal try counts into the timing entry and usage sidecar.**
  - `recordPhaseOutcome` threads `PhaseOutcome.Tokens` into `phase-timing.json` and `<phase>-usage.json` beside `cost_usd`.
  - Legacy sidecars without tokens still parse with zero-valued `Tokens` through `omitempty`.
  - Pinned by `TestRecordPhaseOutcome_ProjectsTokensToSidecarAndTiming` and `TestSidecar_LegacyWithoutTokensParses`.
- **`writeCatalog`'s overflow bucket points to `phase-inventory.json` instead of enumerating names.** When the catalog exceeds `maxEnrichedCatalogCards`, overflow cards omit names and bare lists. A one-line pointer to `phase-inventory.json` preserves selectability without prompt bloat. Pinned by `TestWriteCatalog_OverflowNotEnumerated`, `TestWriteCatalog_PointerLinePresent`, `TestWriteCatalog_PointerLineRequired_Negative` and `TestWriteCatalog_NoOverflow_NoPointer`.
- **The advisor's Plan path writes its raw plan through the artifact-completion contract, not stdout.**
  - The brain writes `routing-plan.json` and the bridge reads it back, matching every phase agent and replacing stdout scraping.
  - The orchestrator persists its clamped result as `phase-plan.json`, so raw proposal and clamped decision both survive.
  - Pinned by `TestPhaseAdvisor_PlanParsesArray`.
- **A stub persona in a prompt test cannot see a real schema conflict.**
  - Tests injecting `WithPersona("PERSONA BODY")` miss the response-schema example inside `agents/evolve-router.md`.
  - When that example omits a field (for example `{cli,tier}`) before the Go-appended example, the prompt shows two schemas and the field drops.
  - `realRouterPersona` loads `agents/evolve-router.md` through `runtime.Caller` from the worktree so goldens test the shipped persona.
  - Pinned by `TestComposePlanPrompt_RealPersonaExistingExampleCarriesTierAndCLI` and `TestRealPersonaFrontmatterOutputFormatEnumeratesTierCLI`.
- **The audit-binding verdict-cache write is a projection, not a second record, and fails closed.**
  - `recordAuditBinding` projects the audit verdict into the content-addressed verdict cache keyed by the binding's worktree tree SHA.
  - It writes only for `verdictcache.Reusable` (never FAIL or SKIPPED) when `verdictcache.ProbeEligible` passes.
  - That probe reads the worktree's own base commit, never `projectRoot` HEAD, which diverges when sibling lanes ship mid-cycle.
  - Writes fail closed while lookups fail open: a poisoned Put affects future consumers, so missing bases or write errors skip Put.
  - `worktreeContentSHA` computes both `WorktreeTreeSHA` and the cache key identically.
- **`buildSelfCheck` is a false-green backstop because a builder's self-report can lie.**
  - A builder can break a unit test, pass its own ACS check, and hand off green; without this check, audit finds it later.
  - Running changed packages' unit tests after build records ground truth for audit and retro.
  - It runs unit tests only (no `-tags integration`) so flaky live-launch tests stay out while real regressions fail.
  - Like `build-gofmt` and `build-derived-regen`, this runs deterministically, uses best effort, and never aborts; audit stays verdict authority.
- **The build-entry graduation guard can abort, unlike the WARN-only `buildSelfCheck`.**
  - A new package with no committed files at HEAD and absent from `go/.apicover-enforce` FAILS build with `abort_reason`.
  - Enrollment is a shipping duty the builder must finish, not a defect left for audit.
  - `buildGraduationCheck` reuses `ciparity.NewUngraduatedPackages` over the same changed-path list as `buildSelfCheck`.
  - It fails open (empty reason) when the worktree is empty or the enforce list is unreadable.
- **A graduation abort message is the remediation interface, not just a diagnosis.**
  - `buildGraduationCheck` spells out two copy-paste edits per ungraduated package: the line for `go/.apicover-enforce` and the `apicover_named_test.go` path.
  - It names the repo-wide enforce list (never the per-cycle ACS coverage gate) because builders lack the ADR context.
  - Multiple flagged packages each get their own two edits.
  - Pinned by `TestBuildGraduationCheck_MessageEmitsTheExactTwoEdits`, `TestBuildGraduationCheck_MessageIsPerPackage` and `TestBuildGraduationCheck_MessageDistinguishesTheTwoApicoverGates`.
- **No raw `carryover_summary` writer can exist below `EVOLVE_PHASE_IO=enforce`.** A populated carryover backlog must stay out of `Context["carryover_summary"]` on off/shadow requests, keeping both that key and `PhaseInput` zero. A carryover byte cap bounds the typed envelope and permits no legacy Context write. Pinned by `TestPlanCycle_CarryoverSummary_NoRawWriterBelowEnforce`.
- **`worktreeContentSHA` binds declared content only, never untracked residue.**
  - Declared content means tracked files, staged additions from `git add`, tracked disk changes, and paths in build and TDD reports.
  - Since F43 part 2 (2026-09-28) it calls `shipmanifest.TakeShipTree` after `git add -u`, read through `gitReadIn` over `gitCapture`.
  - It takes `cs.WorkspacePath` and returns empty without one, because without reports the ship tree is unknown.
  - Unstaged untracked files (reproducers, coverage files, scratch files) stay out of the audit binding and verdict-cache key.
  - It avoids `HEAD^{tree}` because omitting unstaged tracked edits makes dirty lanes bind a base-identical SHA.
  - A residue-only worktree keeps its base identity exactly, because `ProbeEligible` uses non-base identity to detect cacheability.
  - Pinned by `TestWorktreeContentSHA_ExcludesUnrelatedUntrackedResidue`, `TestWorktreeContentSHA_StagesDeclaredNewFile`, `TestWorktreeContentSHA_CapturesUnstagedTrackedModification`, `TestWorktreeContentSHA_ResidueOnlyWorktreeKeepsBaseIdentity`, `TestEmitPhaseBindings_AuditBindingTreeExcludesUnrelatedResidue`, `TestWorktreeContentSHA_BindsTheTreeTheAuditSealed`, `TestWorktreeContentSHA_WithoutAWorkspaceBindsNothing` and `TestEmitPhaseBindings_AuditBindingHoldsTheDeclaredUntrackedDeliverable`.
- **`phaseAgentName` is a static phase→agent name table, not a phase-package lookup.**
  - `core` cannot import `internal/phases/*` without an import cycle, so this table resolves `.evolve/profiles/<agent>.json` inside `core`.
  - Each value strips `evolve-` from `AgentPromptName()`, matching `phases/runner/runner.go`.
  - A phase with no LLM agent (for example ship) is absent; a miss yields nil, which `ValidatePin` passes through.
  - Since L1b, `phaseProfile` checks this table then `<phase>.json`, so minted phases resolve while unprofiled phases yield nil (2026-10-06, ADR-0119).
  - Pinned by `TestPhaseAgentName_ValuesResolveToCheckedInProfiles`, asserting every value resolves to a real profile file on disk.
- **Threading the cycle goal into the advisor's `RouteInput.GoalText` is the precondition for goal-aware planning** — selecting or minting a design phase. Pinned by `TestOrchestrator_ThreadsGoalTextToPlanner`.
- **Routing `Stage=Shadow` computes and ledgers a routing decision every iteration but never overrides the static state machine's phase transitions.** Pinned by `TestOrchestrator_Shadow_LogsTesterInsert_StaticPathUnchanged`.
- **A trivial-cycle digest makes `tdd` skippable; the kernel-validated override (`CanTransition` + `SpineSatisfiedUpTo`) is what adopts the router's scout→build shortcut.** Pinned by `TestOrchestrator_Enforce_TrivialCycle_SkipsOptionalMiddle`.
- **Phase-timing entries stamp each phase's archetype (plan/build/evaluate/control) from `phasespec`.** Using that single source lets roll-ups bucket time into productive, checking, planning, and recovery. Pinned by `TestPhaseTiming_ArchetypeClassified`.
- **Every `PhaseRequest` carries a run id across `RunCycle`, resume, and failure-learning retro.** The bridge uses it to mint run-scoped tmux sessions and attribute ownership. Pinned by `TestCB5_EveryDispatchedPhaseCarriesRunID`, `TestCB5_ResumePathCarriesRunID` and `TestCB5_FailureLearningRetroCarriesRunID`.
- **The per-cycle challenge token** (8-byte hex minted in `RunCycle`) passes through `Context["challengeToken"]`. Scout consumes it to bind evals and anti-replay fallback. Pinned by `TestRunCycle_MintsChallengeToken` and `TestRunCycle_PreservesSuppliedChallengeToken`.
- **Audit/TDD-phase build-artifact churn recovery.**
  - When a guarded phase dirties only `go/evolve` or `go/bin/evolve` in the main tree, the orchestrator discards it, warns, and continues.
  - Other dirty main-tree paths abort through the tree-diff guard and never revert operator files.
  - A TDD tracked-file leak moves into the active worktree and restores main to HEAD.
  - Pinned by `TestOrchestrator_AuditLeakRecover` (`binary_churn_recovered`, `non_binary_leak_aborts`, `mixed_leak_still_aborts`) and `TestTDDLeakRecover`.
- **cwd vs write axis.**
  - Every phase subprocess runs with cwd set to the cycle worktree, but write permissions still key off `worktreePhase`/`WorktreePhase` and `writes_source`.
  - An advisor-minted phase defaults to writable unless `writes_source:false` is set; cwd alone grants no write permission.
  - If worktree provisioning fails in a sequential cycle, phases run with `Worktree=""` and the role-gate blocks writes loudly.
  - A fleet lane defers instead (see `LaneDeferral`), because the bridge refuses fleet launches with no worktree.
  - Pinned by `TestCB1_EveryDispatchedPhaseCarriesWorktree`, `TestCB1_WriteAxisUnchanged`, `TestCB1_ProvisioningFailureDegradesToEmptyWorktree`, `TestInsertedPhaseWritableInheritsWorktree` and `TestInsertedReadOnlyPhaseDoesNotGetWorktree`.
- **`WorktreePhase` reads `phasecontract.SourceWriterPhases` (build, tdd, `test-amplification`, `bug-reproduction`).**
  - The role guard lets those phases write into the worktree, and `shipmanifest.ReportFiles` reads each report.
  - `TestSourceWriterPhases_AreTheRealTreeCatalogsSourceWriters` fails if the list drifts from `writes_source` specs.
  - Pinned also by `TestWorktreePhase` and `TestRole_EverySourceWriterPhaseMayWriteItsLaneWorktreeAndAReaderMayNot`.
- **`discardMainLeak` requires a tracked path**: it shells out to `git checkout HEAD`, so it errors on an untracked file even if gitignored. Pinned by `TestDiscardGitignore`.
- **Phase-outcome single chokepoint.**
  - Every terminal phase dispatch (advance, retry exhaustion, bad verdict, gate reject, tree abort, ledger failure) records once.
  - It records `PhasesRun`, `phase-timing.json`, and `<phase>-usage.json` with the phase's verdict (never invented PASS) and non-empty `abort_reason` on abort.
  - Pinned by `TestPhaseOutcome_TreeGuardAbort_RecordsBuildOutcome`, `TestPhaseOutcome_AbortPaths_AlwaysRecordTimingAndUsage`, `TestPhaseOutcome_NeverInventsPass` and `TestPhaseOutcome_SingleChokepoint_OneRecordPerDispatch`.
- **Control-plane recovery routing.** A `CONTROL_PLANE_VIOLATION` ship error recovers through BUILD (diff owner) instead of re-audit, preventing an infinite loop. Pinned by `TestRunCycle_ShipControlPlaneViolation_RebuildsThenShips`.
- **Deterministic failure-learning floor.** When retro degrades (runner error or non-canonical verdict), the orchestrator writes `retrospective-report.md` and a lesson YAML under `.evolve/instincts/lessons/`. Pinned by `TestRecordFailureLearning_RetroRunnerError_WritesDeterministicArtifacts` and `TestRecordFailureLearning_NonCanonicalVerdict_WritesDeterministicArtifacts`.
- **`optionalInfraSkip` checks the configured mandatory phase set, not a hardcoded ship check.** Any mandatory phase stays protected from infra skip even when Optional in its catalog spec. Pinned by `TestMandatoryPhaseNeverInfraSkipped` and `TestShipNeverInfraSkipped`.
- **A phase minted at cycle start becomes both dispatchable and routable with no recompile** — the same path a built-in phase takes. Pinned by `TestRegisterMintedPhases_MakesPhaseDispatchableAndRoutable`.
- **`recordPhaseOutcome` derives the context-fill projection at one chokepoint.**
  - `phaseOutcomeFrom` copies `ResolvedModel` (tier) and `Tokens` from `PhaseResponse`, then `recordPhaseOutcome` calls `contextfill.WindowSizeForTier(out.ResolvedModel)`.
  - Unresolvable tiers return `contextfill.ErrInvalidWindow` and leave `ContextFillRatio` and `ContextWindowHot` at zero instead of guessing.
  - Pinned by `TestRecordPhaseOutcome_ProjectsContextFillWhenTierResolvable`, `TestRecordPhaseOutcome_ColdPhaseIsNotFlaggedHot`, `TestRecordPhaseOutcome_UnresolvableTierLeavesContextFillZero`, `TestRecordPhaseOutcome_ZeroTokensRecordsZeroFillNotHot` and `TestPhaseOutcomeFrom_CarriesTierProvenanceAndTokens`.
- **`RetroDecision` is prefix+suffix composed.** Without `disposition.json`, the disposition gate prepends `disposition-gate:` and keeps the branch decision (for example `proceed:`) as suffix. Pinned by `TestOrchestrator_RetroPASS_DoesNotRouteToShip`.
- **Recurring audit-fail disposition has two modes**: strict mode blocks on 2 distinct `code-audit-fail` records; fluent mode (the default) proceeds with awareness and ends the cycle. Pinned by `TestOrchestrator_RetroFAIL_RecurringAudit_FluentEnd`.
- **A debugger serving a fleet-rebase recovery returns its tree to `resumeFleetRebaseAfterDebugger`.** The tree commits on the fork point, rebases, pends, and routes through `routeRebasedExplanation`. Other recoveries honor the debugger's target (`ship_recovery_debugger.go`).
- **The fleet-rebase recovery** (`recoverFromShipError`, `ship_recovery.go`, ADR-0049, ADR-0105). It is a chain of steps with no LLM.
  - A contention error (a sibling lane moved main) waits a jittered backoff before the recovery, so lanes in lockstep do not collide again. Other errors recover at once.
  - The pre-screen `ClassifyFleetRebaseCandidate` runs before the replay. A candidate that main already holds ends with no replay and no audit. A pre-screen error does not stop the recovery: the rebase runs and reports its own failure.
  - A predicted conflict gets no unwind. The rebase finds the same conflict, and one code path routes it to the debugger.
  - The rebase replays the lane onto the new main. A conflict only in generated projections (for example `control-flags.md`) is regenerated from the merged source. A conflict on any other path is overlapping work and goes to the debugger.
  - After a real unwind, the recovery pends the change whatever the replay did. An aborted replay leaves the carrier, and the pend restores the audited shape that the debugger and a re-ship expect.
  - A legacy cycle (no explanation contract) tries RUNG 2, and else goes to Audit with a WARN that names the reason. RUNG 2 reviews only the hunks that intersect, and its resolution must match the audited patch-id (`TestRecoverFromShipError_ALegacyCleanRebaseIsAuditedAgainForAStatedReason`, `TestRunCycleFromPhase_ALegacyRebaseWhoseMainMovedIsAuditedAgainForAStatedReason`).
  - RUNG 0 (`compositionCarryForward`, the `trivial-rebase` carry) is retired since 2026-10-21. Its snapshot diffed `main...git_head`, and `git_head` is the tip of main when the audit was bound, not the audited change. Thus it did not carry a real rebase. The live ledger held 45 carry records, and all were `identical-rebase` records of the identity carry (ADR-0105 B3). Every new cycle has an explanation contract, so the identity carry serves the callers of RUNG 0.
  - The recovery calls `router.Recover` directly, so it works when no routing strategy is wired. Every target of the recovery chain is a legal edge from ship in the default graph (`TestRecover_EveryRecoveryRouteIsALegalEdgeFromShip`). The recovery still refuses an illegal edge, because `WithLegalGraph` can change the graph (`TestRecoverFromShipError_ARouteTheStateMachineForbidsAborts`).
- **The rebased explanation** (`routeRebasedExplanation`). It moves the base binding to the fork point. When the host proves the change byte-identical, the approved Build stays: the carry ships it, else Audit runs again. Otherwise the snapshot is invalidated and Build writes the explanation again. Only a change pending on the fork point is rebound, because Audit reads `git diff HEAD`. A committed change goes to Build, whose normalisation pends it.
- **The unwind** (`unwindBeforeFleetRebase`, `ship_recovery_unwind.go`). It takes ship's inbox consumption out of the change that the rebase replays, so the rebased change is the one that Audit reviewed. `unwindShipCommit` puts one carrier commit of the audited tree on the audited base, or says why it declined and changes nothing. `consumptionDecline` holds only when HEAD differs from the audited tree by whole consumption pairs. A pair is an item removed from the inbox root and written to `consumed/` under the same name.
- **A continuation lane reaches the identity proof** (ADR-0128, plan component Q12). The B1 unwind declines for a consumed item that released a continuation, and `unwindBeforeFleetRebase` returns `unwindKeepsConsumption`, because the pointer is only in ship's commit. After a clean rebase, the recovery pends ship's replayed commit on the fork point. The rebind (`explanationdocs.RebindIdenticalRebase`) does not count ship's consumption in the identity, and the released continuation stays in the pending change. The identity carry declines that tree, because ship re-proves the carry on the bytes, so the route is Audit, not Build. When the proof cannot run, `routeRebasedExplanation` writes `identity proof skipped` and emits `ORCHESTRATOR_IDENTITY_PROOF_SKIPPED` with `fields.reason`, not "not proven identical".
- **`ResumeOptions` has seams with production defaults.** `MainAhead` counts the commits of `origin/main` past a base (`git rev-list --count`). `Now` is the clock, `PathExists` is `os.Stat`, and `Log` takes the discovery lines (discarded when nil). `MaxAge` comes from policy `resume.max_checkpoint_age_h` when it is zero.
  - `AllowHeadMoved` waives the age check. `LoadResumeState` sets it when the operator names the state file in `EVOLVE_CYCLE_STATE_FILE`, which is the deliberate override. No env flag sets it (`EVOLVE_RESUME_ALLOW_HEAD_MOVED` was retired in cycle 15).
- **The orchestrator listens to the Signal Center (`signal.go`, ADR-0101 S1).**
  - `recordPhaseOutcome` is the first producer. Listeners observe and never decide; `observeSignal` reports evidence while floors keep verdicts and halts.
  - Lock order: the Center releases its lock during delivery, so `observeSignal` takes `signalMu` only and never calls `Emit`.
  - `signalMu` keeps `SignalSummary` safe during concurrent drain, pinned under `-race` by `TestSignalSummary_IsSafeToReadWhileTheCenterDelivers`.
  - A WARN or FAIL `phase.outcome` or `cycle.sealed` event uses one stderr-sink format instead of per-chokepoint hand formatting.
- **`signal_cycle.go` adapters convert existing typed values into Events at owning chokepoints.** They emit `cycle.sealed` and `system.failure` at `completeCycle` (and abnormal epilogue), `ship.error` at `recordShipError`, and `quota.paused` at `pauseForQuota`. Producers observe; decisions stay put.
- **A phase's WARN/FAIL `phase.outcome` event carries its error-severity diagnostic codes** in `fields.diagnostic_codes`, comma-joined; an uncoded FAIL carries no such field. Pinned by `TestEmitPhaseOutcome_FailCarriesTheDiagnosticCodes` and `TestEmitPhaseOutcome_UncodedFailCarriesNoCodesField`.
- **`emitShipError` projects only the triage whitelist (`shiperr.SignalDebugKeys`) from `ShipError.Debug`.** Non-whitelisted keys and empty values stay out. Pinned by `TestEmitShipError_ProjectsSignalDebugKeys`.
- **The orchestrator holds the ship-window lease only for a fleet-rebase recovery** (`shipwindow_hold.go`, since 2026-10-19). An audit PASS takes no lease, because the ship phase takes it over its binding check and its push ([internal-phases-ship](internal-phases-ship.md)). `TestRecordAndBranch_AnAuditPassLeavesTheShipWindowFreeForASibling` (integration tier) pins it.
  - `recoverFromShipError` and `resumeFleetRebaseAfterDebugger` take the lease (`holdShipWindow`) before the rebase onto `main`. The lease then covers the rebase, the composed-tree gates of the identity carry or the composition carry, and the re-ship.
  - `keepShipWindowOnlyForShip` keeps the lease only for a route to Ship. A route to Audit, Build or the end releases it, so no LLM phase holds it.
  - `runPhase` runs the ship in a context marked with `shipwindow.WithHeld`, so the ship does not take the lease again. It releases the lease when the ship returns. `RunCycle` and the resume loop also release it on exit.
  - A canceled wait WARNs and the recovery goes on unleased. A break of a live holder at the TTL emits `ORCHESTRATOR_SHIP_WINDOW_LEASE_BROKEN`.
  - The lease beats while the orchestrator holds it ([internal-shipwindow](internal-shipwindow.md), D4), so a recovery longer than the TTL keeps it. A lease that the holder lost emits `ORCHESTRATOR_SHIP_WINDOW_LEASE_BROKEN` with `fields.role=holder`, and the re-ship sees the loss at its push check. `WithShipWindowOptions` injects the clock and the ticker for tests. `defaultShipWindowTicker` is a package variable like `backoffSleep`. `TestMain` sets it to an inert ticker once, so that the many orchestrator tests that never set `WithShipWindowOptions` start no real beat.
  - Pinned also by `TestShipWindow_TwoLanesWithSectionsLongerThanTTLNeverOverlap` and `TestHoldShipWindow_ALostLeaseSignalsTheHolderAndTheReShipSeesIt`.
  - Pinned by `TestRecoverFromShipError_HoldsTheShipWindowFromTheRebaseThroughTheReShip`, `TestRecoverFromShipError_ARebaseRoutedToAuditReleasesTheShipWindow`, `TestRunPhase_OnlyTheShipRunsInsideAHeldWindowAndReleasesIt`, `TestKeepShipWindowOnlyForShip_KeepsTheHoldOnlyForAReShip`, `TestHoldShipWindow_ACanceledWaitRecoversUnleasedWithAWarn`, `TestHoldShipWindow_BreakingAHungHolderSignals` and `TestReleaseShipWindow_AReleaseFailureWarnsAndClearsTheHold`.

## Core file group 3 rationale

The reasons that the comments of the decision, disposition, dossier, failure-learning, lane-scope, leak-recovery, scoped-merge and mint-exemption files carried. The code and the tests state the what.

- **Decision-branch reason strings are a contract.**
  - The reason prefix (`proceed:`, `retry-with-fallback:` and the rest) is grepped by dashboards and scenario pins.
  - When the advisor agrees with the deterministic decision, the deterministic reason string is kept.
  - A rerun target clamps to the upstream phases (audit, build, tdd). Re-shipping is the dedicated RESHIP action. Thus `rerun_phase: ship` must not become a reship that skips the precondition.
  - A nil validation plan writes `[]`, so a validated-clean plan differs from validation that never ran.
- **Disposition vocabulary.**
  - An out-of-vocabulary value is rejected with the offending field named, so a document that parses as JSON still fails the gate.
  - The routing term is `console`, not `escalate`: one vocabulary with the route field of ADR-0074 and the plan-time gate.
  - The seeded skeleton leaves `Reason` empty, so the auditor's upgrade is a value edit and not a shape edit.
  - Seeding never clobbers an existing disposition file.
  - The defect-ledger gate is the loud enforcement.
- **Dossier and digest identity.**
  - The dossier producer takes phase timings as a parameter. `RunCycle` writes `phase-timing.json` in a deferred call that lands after the producer. A re-read then finds no phases.
  - The dossier builder has no `ContractVerifier`, so `DeliverableValid` stays false and the live floor owns reconcile.
  - `backfillFailReasons` reads only orchestrator-authored phase-timing state, never an agent-writable file, so a failure identity cannot be forged from the workspace.
  - Display and identity are two projections of one reason string. The digest, the dossier and `audit-fail-reason.json` keep the reason verbatim. Only the hash input is normalized.
  - The fingerprint carries the phase as a prefix and as a hash input, so two failures that differ only in phase never share an id.
  - The boilerplate router and epilogue templates are single sources shared with their detectors, so the writer and the content-free check cannot drift.
  - The boilerplate patterns are anchored at both ends, so appended text makes a reason content-bearing.
  - Cause chains grow prefix-first, so the cause head is tail-kept.
- **Failure advisor trust boundary.**
  - The model sees the neutralized pane, but the promoted `pane_substr` is matched against RAW panes. A quoted neutralization artifact then promotes a signature that can never fire.
  - `parseFailureAdvice` applies the checks of `recovery.PromoteAdvice` early, so a bad verdict fails at the parse with the raw output in the error.
  - The hook scans the agent-stripped pane, so a novel wedge that quotes a seeded signature in diff content is not read as already classified.
  - It passes an empty prompt on purpose: the echo half is neutered by the protect list.
  - A pane the registry already classifies gets no LLM consultation.
  - The advise timeout is long enough to learn one novel signature. Each promotion saves about 20 minutes of `maxExtends` burn. The abort never hangs on a wedged advisor.
  - A composition-root enforce flip with no adviser wired skips the advise-then-promote path silently. Thus `FailureAdviserWired` exists.
- **Failure learning.**
  - `phaseOutcomeFrom` records a canonical agent verdict as-is and synthesizes FAIL for anything else, so a synthesized PASS is structurally impossible.
  - `recordPhaseOutcome` is the one chokepoint, so `PhasesRun`, `phase-timing.json` and `<phase>-usage.json` agree.
  - The learning gate orders incomplete, canceled and quota-deferred.
  - A cancellation is an operator stop, and an all-families quota exhaustion is a resume checkpoint: neither is evidence that the phase failed.
  - The cancel check comes BEFORE the quota sentinel.
  - A ship dispatch explanation is preserved for the coherence floor even when the quota boundary skips learning.
  - A missing persona doc is a known configuration absence and learns deterministically with no LLM dispatch.
  - `completeRetro` is a divergent twin of `phaseCompletionRecord.persist`, kept and named. A PASS retro must still deliver a valid disposition that agrees with the digest. If not, the completion surfaces a gate reason.
  - An out-of-band retro keeps the no-main-tree-cwd invariant.
  - The recorder and the learning engine are built lazily for an orchestrator assembled as a literal. The clock goes through a closure and the Center through an accessor. Tests swap `o.now` and apply `WithSignalCenter` after construction.
- **Final-verdict floor.**
  - Only an authoritative phase (the resolved ship floor plus ship) sets `FinalVerdict`.
  - A later non-floor phase must not clobber it: its non-PASS outcome is appended to `VerdictsNotAdopted`.
  - A non-floor phase degrades to SKIPPED with a WARN only after the floor passed.
  - Before that, an unparseable verdict of scout, triage or intent stays cycle-fatal.
- **Lost-landing floor.**
  - An absent or unreadable `ship-error.json` means ship never ran.
  - Any other reading invents a landing loss on every pre-ship cycle.
- **Lane scope.**
  - `scoutArtifactName` derives from the phasecontract registry. A frozen copy degrades goal-hash normalization into its fail-open branch when the registry moves. That is a lane-identity bug that looks like an absent report.
  - `LaneScope` is an alias of the leaf declaration because exactly one non-test file may declare the wire tags.
  - A broken or empty pin falls back to the legacy env fleet scope with a WARN. It never aborts. It never yields a silently empty contract.
  - The scope is pinned to disk BEFORE any phase output exists.
  - The LAST fenced JSON block with a `goal_hash` wins, since the Decision Trace is the final block of a report.
- **Leak recovery.**
  - The deliverable-path test matches a directory prefix but needs exact equality for a file, so a look-alike name cannot false-positive.
  - `-uall` lists every untracked file, so leaks relocate at file granularity.
  - A tracked-edit relocation assumes the worktree copy is at HEAD (the caller checks), so it never clobbers independent work.
  - `git checkout HEAD -- p` resets index and tree, so a staged-only leak is discarded too.
  - A non-zero git exit comes back as `(c != 0, e == nil)`. The code branches on it. It never wraps a nil error, because that renders `<nil>` and breaks `errors.Is`.
  - `git check-ignore -q` exits 0 when ignored and 1 when not.
  - A launch error on `git diff --quiet` counts as not clean, so the caller takes the conservative discard path.
  - `moveFile` copies and removes when `os.Rename` fails with EXDEV across mounts.
  - Build artifacts a builder may rebuild into the main tree, non-deliverable `.evolve/` state at any depth, and bare directory entries from `-uall` are legitimate.
  - The evolve deliverable prefixes (`evals/`, `phases/`, `commit-prefix-scope.json`) flow only through worktree-based phases and must never escape.
- **Mint exemption.**
  - Registry names are attacker-influenceable path segments, so the kebab-case floor runs BEFORE any filesystem access, and a forged `../x` never leaves `.evolve/phases/`.
  - The spec read is capped at 1 MiB against a planted giant file.
  - A mint writes EXACTLY `phase.json`: a companion payload still fires the guard.
  - With the OS sandbox off, any on-disk `phase.json` is forgeable, so the exemption trusts clamp parity and not provenance.
  - It reads after `Lstat`, so a FIFO, a symlink or an oversized file is never read.
- **Scoped merge review (rung 2).**
  - The reviewer is injected so the core stays pure, and a nil reviewer leaves the rung dark.
  - An empty intersection is compatible and wastes no review.
  - A malformed diff or hunk header fails closed: a non-nil error, no reviewer call, no partial result.
  - A resolution is trusted by recomputed patch-id and never on the reviewer's word.
  - A zero-count old-side range is a unit-width point, so an insertion still overlaps a change that spans it.
  - `scopedReviewMethod` mirrors the ledger constant because core cannot import the ledger adapter, and the two strings must stay identical.
- **Errors.**
  - `ErrArtifactTimeout` covers exit 81.
  - Exit 127 (a missing binary) is NOT transient: an absent CLI is an environment defect and must fail loud.
  - `isArtifactTimeout` must never widen to the teardown union: it feeds only the exit-code and delivery-cause attribution of the failure-diagnostic writer.
  - `ErrUnsafeConfig` marks a flow that can ship without the integrity floor (ADR-0060).
- **Git seam.**
  - `gitRunner` is the one git-execution seam of the package, so tests fake git.
  - A dirty-path error propagates so the guard degrades to snapshot missed and never misreports leaks.
- **Identity carry-forward (ADR-0105 B3).**
  - After the rebind proved the pended change identical, the carry proves the same on the trees and runs the composed-tree gates under the fence.
  - Any doubt returns false and the change is audited again.
  - The gates count only when they ran on the tree that ship commits.
- **Judgment lessons.**
  - `plan-review` is listed although it has no catalog phase today, so the lesson works the day it becomes one.
- **ADR pointers moved here:**
  - ADR-0039 (audit-declared envelope), ADR-0044 (failure advisor and recorder), ADR-0045, ADR-0048 (shadow stage), ADR-0052 (agent identity), ADR-0072 (failure decision and dossier).

## Core file group 4 rationale

The reasons that the comments of the orchestrator, phase advisor, phase binding, self-check and graduation files carried. The code and the tests state the what.

- **Orchestrator wiring.**
  - `WithStageEvidence` wires the `StageRecorder` and `StageSealer` ports of shadow-contract K2. `RunCycle` and the resume entry open one recorder session when the cycle number is known.
