Both close and seal it in a `defer` on every exit path, so a failed, parked or cancelled cycle seals too. A hard kill is the one exit that cannot seal. The orphan seal (K2f) is to repair it.
    - The seal runs on a context without the cancel of the cycle, bounded by `stageSealBudget`. The `exit` of the seal is `completed`, `failed`, `parked`, `deadline` or `unended`.
    - A seal that fails emits `ORCHESTRATOR_STAGE_EVIDENCE_FAULT` and never changes the verdict of the cycle. A cycle with no decision seals nothing and stays quiet.
    - `emitLadderDecision` is the emit site of the key `workflow.retry_ladder.stage`. It records the fields of the signal and `would_stop`.
  - `PhaseBoundaryCheckpointer` and `QuotaBoundaryCheckpointer` are package-level hooks set by the checkpoint package, because core cannot import it without a cycle.
  - `specFor` canonicalizes the phase name before the lookup, so the core and router spellings of one phase cannot miss each other.
    - The registry is the single source of truth and wins over `builtinControlSpec`.
    - `builtinControlSpec` only supplies the debugger's signal-driven successor for a phase with no registry entry.
  - `successorStrategy` degrades to `literalSuccessorStrategy` on a catalog miss. Config selects among branches the state machine already deems legal and never invents one.
  - A `PhaseMinter` must return a spec with `Optional=true`. A minted phase can never satisfy or displace the build, audit and ship floor.
  - `CycleRequest.Env` and `Context` are copied, so a mutation after `RunCycle` starts never reaches an in-flight or completed run.
  - `legacyExplanationTestStorage` is package-private on purpose. Only core's same-package legacy fake may opt out of the fresh-cycle explanation contract.
    - `explanationContractVersion` has no exported option, so production callers cannot downgrade the mandatory contract.
  - `gitHEAD` is not evidence that this cycle shipped, because a fleet sibling can move HEAD too. The outcome label reads the ship latch, and a HEAD read error counts as "no movement".
  - `gitDirtyPaths` feeds the tree-diff guard, which snapshots it before and after each source-writing phase to catch a leak that escaped the sandbox.
  - `safetyViolations` is computed once at construction over the wired state machine, config and catalog. A non-empty result makes `RunCycle` and `RunCycleFromPhase` fail closed with `ErrUnsafeConfig` before any phase runs, so an unsafe registry edit can never run a phase.
  - `shipFloor` can only relax build and tdd. The router self-seals the non-removable evaluator regardless.
  - `signalMu` is the first production mutex in core. The Signal Center holds no lock while delivering, `observeSignal` never emits, and no orchestrator path holds `signalMu` across an `Emit`.
  - `currentRunID` is read atomically by the construction-time stamping ledger on every append, so goroutine-spawning observers read the run identity race-free. An empty value means no run is in flight and entries are not stamped.
  - `WithWorktreeBase` and `WithWorktreeProvisioner` both set `o.worktree`. Production uses only the base override and tests use only the fake.
  - `CatalogPublisherWired` exists because without a publisher a mid-cycle mint never reaches the live contract resolver.
  - `HasRunner` is the composition root's read seam, because a phase the router nominates without a runner is silently skipped by the missing-runner escape hatch.
- **Nil wiring defaults.** Each optional collaborator has a defined meaning when unset.
  - A nil `planner` gives no advisor plan, so the kernel floor falls back to the never-skip spine. The planner is consulted once at cycle start, only at `Stage>=Advisory`, and its output is always clamped to the integrity floor.
  - A nil `reviewer` is `noopReviewer`: every non-error, non-SKIPPED verdict is a success. When a reviewer returns `Approve=false`, the cycle aborts with its reason.
  - A nil `observer` is `noopObserver`. `Start` is called once before each `runner.Run` and the returned cancel runs once after.
  - A nil `contractVerifier` gives the salvage rung zero budget, so the correction ladder degrades to redispatch only.
  - A nil `failureAdviser` leaves the hook inert. It is consulted only at `cfg.PhaseRecovery == StageEnforce`, and only for unclassified artifact-timeout panes.
  - A nil `directivesProvider` means no directives. The provider is fail-open: it returns a possibly empty set and never an error.
  - The identity carry and RUNG 2 never fire unless all three composition seams are set. A nil `scopedMergeReviewer` leaves RUNG 2 dark.
  - A nil `catalogRefresh` skips the refresh, and a refresh error only WARNs and never blocks the cycle.
  - A nil `modelCatalogLookup` skips the resolvability gate, while guardrail validation still runs.
  - A nil `retryAdjudicator` is a supported production state: policy alone is enough authority to grant a retry.
  - A nil `failureCountFor` disables retry tier escalation, and a nil `continuationFor` means continuations never adopt.
  - The retry tier escalation counts the ids of the cycle claims (`processingClaimIDs`). An unreadable claim file or claim dir prints one WARN line on the stderr of the orchestrator, and the readable ids still count (`TestProcessingClaimIDs_NamesAnUnreadableClaimFile`, `TestProcessingClaimIDs_AnUnreadableClaimDirIsAWarnLine`).
  - An empty `catalog` means only built-in phases exist. A nil `registrar` ignores `MintPhases`.
- **Ordering in `NewOrchestrator` and `RunCycle`.**
  - The outcome recorder reads the clock and the catalog live, because tests swap `o.now` after construction and mints change the catalog mid-cycle.
  - The failure-learning engine is built after `o.carry`, because it mints through the one wired carryover lifecycle.
  - The state machine receives its config-driven verdict-branch resolution only after the options settle the catalog. An empty catalog or spine order degrades to the literal table.
  - The run-id stamping decorator wraps the ledger exactly once, at construction.
  - `newCycleRun` returns a cleanup closure that `RunCycle` defers in its own frame, so the exit actions fire at cycle exit in LIFO order.
  - `cycleRun` is the one addressable home for the dispatch loop's state. Late mutations by sub-methods stay visible to the exit defers and the next iteration.
  - The exit defers keep these rules.
    - The worktree preserve decision reads `cr.preserveWorktree` and `cr.cycleCompletedNormally` at defer-execution time.
    - No exit path leaves a started cycle without its evidence trail.
    - A graceful interrupt resumes the phase that was active. That defer runs before the abnormal epilogue marks the cycle aborted.
  - The fresh-cycle marker is persisted before the first phase. A crash before Build then cannot turn a new cycle into a legacy exemption from the explanation contract.
  - Phase timings are written in a deferred call that also runs when `RunCycle` returns an error, so partial timing data survives for inspection. Interaction outcomes are recorded on abort paths too.
  - The content-addressed audit-reuse probe is shadow only. It runs before the loop, against a preserved or re-dispatched worktree. A fresh worktree skips the lookup to prevent fresh-base collisions.
  - The loop is labeled `OuterLoop`. The extracted sub-methods signal termination with `loopBreak`, and a bare `break` inside the switch ladder exits the switch and not the loop.
  - Triage continuation adoption may replace both worktree and review base, so the final context is sealed once before the first Build dispatch. Adoption is keyed to the actual claim, since triage claims items into `processing/cycle-N` mid-cycle.
  - The post-scout re-plan fires once, after scout's handoff is recorded and before the next `selectNext`. A re-plan thus cannot widen the run set or bypass the spine gate.
  - A deterministic gate that FAILed gets one bounded builder fix and a same-gate re-run. Nothing downstream is bypassed, and the same gate must pass with the audit and ship floors unchanged.
  - Exhausting the iteration budget records an explicit abort naming the stalled phase, so the escape is diagnosable and not `FAILED_UNEXPLAINED`. It runs before `finalizeCycle`, so the recorded FAIL preserves the worktree for salvage.
- **Phase advisor.**
  - All advisor output is advisory.
    - The pure `router.Route()` clamp re-validates it against the kernel floor (mandatory spine, TDD pin, ship needs a real audit).
    - A hallucinated or malformed proposal thus cannot weaken the ship guarantee.
    - Any failure returns an error and the caller degrades to the deterministic static path.
  - `brain` is built once under a `sync.Once`. A bare nil-check is a read-check-write race for a `PhaseAdvisor` built as a literal and first used concurrently.
    - A nil bridge stays nil, so the legacy "nil bridge" fail-safe survives.
  - `wiredAdvisor` is the one construction site of the leaf (`TestPhaseAdvisor_OneConstructionSite`).
    - `bridgeRequestOf` is the one projection of the leaf's request onto the bridge request.
    - `ChainAttempt` marks each launch as one step of the advisor's own chain walk. A chain-walking bridge handle passes it through and does not walk it again.
  - `WithAdvisorSignals` is construction-time DI. The composition root builds the Signal Center before the advisor in the same function.
  - An empty `WithPersona` falls back to the legacy inline framing as a fail-safe.
    - The CLI and model fallback is deep (opus), because composing a cycle and inventing phases is deep-reasoning work.
    - The composition root normally overrides both from the router profile.
  - `writeArtifactAtomically` writes through a temp file and a rename. A capture artifact is thus either absent or complete.
    - The ledger's disk-read SHA equals the span's in-memory SHA.
    - It is single-writer per workspace and kind per cycle, so the fixed `.tmp` suffix cannot collide.
  - `benchedCLIsForRouting` sorts by family for a deterministic, prompt-prefix-cache-friendly prompt. CLI health is advice and never a planning prerequisite, so an unreadable store yields an empty list.
  - The recon is advisory and fails open.
    - `recentChangeCommits` bounds the git-log window.
    - `reconGitTimeout` bounds the git subprocess, so a hung repo cannot stall plan composition.
    - Duplicate paths are kept, because frequency is the churn signal.
    - The git I/O lives in core and is injected into the leaf.
  - The `Deprecated` test facades (`composePlanPrompt`, `sanitizeAdvisorTier`, `writeCatalog`, `mintConfigsFrom` and the rest) stay because ACS-named tests and the protected guard tests call them. Production goes through `Plan` and `RePlan`.
- **Provenance bindings.**
  - FAIL is bound. Without a binding, ship's lookup has nothing for the run and the FAIL verdict is invisible to the gate built to enforce it.
    - SKIPPED stays excluded, since no audit ran and there is no artifact to bind.
  - The phase-agnostic binding for user and inserted phases is dormant until `EVOLVE_PHASE_IO=enforce`. The default loop thus emits exactly the audit and build bindings it always did.
  - `phaseRole` maps audit to `auditor` and build to `builder`, the strings ship's audit binding (`findLatestAudit`) and the `rt-001-ledger-role-completeness` red-team predicate require. It is total over all phases and pinned by `TestPhaseRole`.
  - `bindingInputs` carries the project root, workspace and worktree explicitly. The typed phase output holds the wire result and not the paths the git probes need.
    - `worktreeBase` is the correct operand for the verdict-cache fresh-base guard.
    - The project root HEAD at audit time can diverge under fleet concurrency.
  - `recordPhaseBinding` delegates audit and build to their specialized recorders unchanged, so their ledger bytes stay identical.
    - The bodies are genuinely asymmetric. Only audit computes a worktree-tree SHA, reads its artifact fatally, derives the exit code from the verdict and projects into the verdict cache.
    - The collapse is thus at the dispatch and role-naming layer and not a merged body.
  - `recordAuditBinding` writes the entry that ship's audit binding requires. `tree_state_sha` is the sha256 of `git diff HEAD`, byte-identical to ship's `computeTreeStateSHA`. `git diff HEAD` exits 1 when differences exist, which is not an error, and only an exit above 1 is fatal. The recorder is best-effort: a failure WARNs, and ship then fails loudly on the missing binding and never ships unbound.
  - The binding tree is the tree ship will commit from the builder-declared index, not the auditor's own `HEAD^{tree}`, which can never equal it. Nothing is committed (`write-tree` only).
  - `worktreeContentSHA` is the single source of three values: the audit binding's `WorktreeTreeSHA`, the composition entries' `TreeStateSHA` and the ADR-0048 verdict-cache key. Every recorded, looked-up and verified value is thus computed identically.
    - It returns an empty string when the worktree or workspace is empty or git fails.
    - Callers degrade: ship falls back to the auditor's value and the cache does not record or match.
    - An in-place worktree is the operator's tree, and a cycle never stages its index.
  - `recordBuildBinding` exists because the orchestrator's per-phase entry has role `build`, the phase name, and not `builder`, the agent name.
    - A cycle that goes through formal audit will otherwise false-FAIL provenance with "no role:builder entry".
    - The binding mirrors the audit one and WARNs loudly without blocking the cycle.
  - `recordGenericBinding` is reached only at `EVOLVE_PHASE_IO=enforce`, gated by `emitPhaseBindings`.
- **Build worktree normalization.**
  - `writeBuildDiffSummary` runs after the soft reset, gofmt and the refresh of the derived files, because each of them changes the diff. It writes the diff metrics and the `## Changes` table of `build-report.md` from `WorktreeBaseSHA` (`internal/builddiff`, [posthoc-schema.md](../posthoc-schema.md)). A failed derivation writes `absent` and a WARN line. A remediation round rewrites the report after the first build binding, so `maybeRemediate` binds the fixed build again (`TestRemediation_TheFixedBuildIsBoundAgainAfterTheHostRewritesItsReport`). Pinned by `TestWriteBuildDiffSummary_ANoOpCycleReportsZeroFilesFromItsBase`.
  - `normalizeWorktreeToBase` soft-resets to the cycle base so a committing builder's work becomes pending again. The builder commits for crash safety, but the auditor and the audit binding inspect the pending diff, which a commit empties. It never aborts the cycle and is a no-op when HEAD already equals the base.
  - A persisted base on the resume path can be stale after the operator rebased the worktree onto a moved main.
    - A soft reset to a non-ancestor repoints the branch and stages the whole delta between histories as a spurious diff.
    - It is thus skipped, and the manual recovery's "as-is" state stands.
  - `normalizeBuildWorktree` is shared by `RunCycle` and `RunCycleFromPhase`. The soft reset runs only after Build. The gofmt normalization runs after every worktree phase, because tdd, build and test-amplification all author Go that the audit gofmt gate scans.
  - An in-place worktree is the operator's tree: no soft reset, no `gofmt -w` and no projection regen.
  - The derived-projection regen is build-only and gated on the flag registry (the single source of truth) actually changing, so non-flag cycles pay no `go run` cost.
  - The covering-test corpus for test-amplification is derived after every worktree phase.
    - A resume past build never sees `PhaseBuild` complete in this process.
    - The phase then silently degrades to an unscoped whole-repo search.
    - The derivation is a no-op once the corpus exists.
  - The selfcheck runs after the regen so the tested tree matches what audit binds. When the build-floor reviewer is enforced, the same selfcheck already ran at the review seam, so the duplicate `go test` pass is skipped.
  - `changedWorktreePaths` covers tracked changes against HEAD and untracked new files. A cycle that adds a file under a projection's source prefix is thus not missed.
    - It splits on newlines and not `strings.Fields`, so a path with spaces stays one entry.
    - A path that is not a git repo yields no paths and no error.
  - `ChangedWorktreePaths` is a projection for `evolve phase verify build`, so the gate and the self-check judge the same diff. A second derivation in `internal/cli/phasecmd` can let them drift (ADR-0034).
  - `normalizeBuildGofmt` applies `gofmt -w -s` before the audit gofmt gate. Formatting is deterministic work and must not depend on the LLM builder remembering it.
    - It is scoped to the same module dir the audit gate scans (`codequality.ModuleDir`), so the two cannot disagree.
    - The worktree is cut from CI-clean main, so only this cycle's files can be dirty.
    - A gofmt failure WARNs and never aborts.
- **Build self-check.**
  - The self-check is best-effort and never aborts the cycle. It clears any prior `.evolve/build-selfcheck.json` first.
    - The toolchain gate (`go/acs/regression/buildselfcheck`) reads that artifact at audit and hard-fails on it.
    - Without the clear, a retry that fixes the build can see the previous run's failures and loop forever.
    - The clear sits above the early return for a cycle with no changed Go packages.
  - `realGoUnitTest` runs unit tests only, with no integration tag.
    - It scrubs the subprocess env with `ipcenv.Scrub`, so the campaign's runtime flags do not flip env-sensitive tests. An inherited `EVOLVE_FLEET=1` can flip `internal/bridge`'s fleet-mode worktree guard.
    - A bounded timeout keeps a wedged test from hanging the build phase.
  - A changed path whose package directory no longer exists means the diff deleted the package, so nothing is left to test. This is checked on the filesystem and not by output text, so a present package keeps its real verdict.
  - `goTestExcludedByBuildTags` separates a package whose files are all excluded by build tags from a real failure.
    - Every cycle materializes a `//go:build`-gated acceptance package (`acs/cycleN`). An untagged `go test` of one prints "build constraints exclude all Go files".
    - Flagging it will WARN every cycle and bury real signal.
    - The `imports ` guard keeps the check narrow. When a transitive dependency is the excluded one, `go test` prints an `imports <dep>:` chain and the tested package genuinely failed.
    - That case is still reported, as are compile errors and assertion failures.
  - A tag-gated package is invisible to the default context, so the tagged runner runs an added package under the tags its files declare.
  - `removeBuildSelfCheckArtifact` treats a missing file, the common case, as no error. A write error on the artifact is non-fatal because the WARN already surfaced it.
- **Graduation check.**
  - `buildGraduationCheck` flags a changed `go/internal/<pkg>` package that is new this cycle and absent from `go/.apicover-enforce`.
    - A modified pre-existing package is the enforce ratchet's concern.
    - A deleted or renamed-away package is not new. Flagging it makes graduation hygiene un-shippable.
    - It fails open when the worktree is empty or the enforce file is unreadable, and for a package it cannot prove new. Audit stays the backstop.
  - A test-only package has no exported production symbol, so the repo-wide apicover gate cannot fire on it. `packageHasProductionGoFiles` delegates to the shared predicate `ciparity.PackageDirHasProductionGoFiles`, so the two seams cannot disagree. The vacuous-obligation skip is announced, so a test-only mint that lands unenrolled shows in the cycle log and not at audit.
  - The abort reason lands on the recorded phase outcome (`AbortReason`), unlike the WARN-only unit-test self-check.
- **Test-suite constraints.**
  - `TestMain` zeroes the retry-backoff sleep for the whole suite, saving about 250 seconds of wall clock across the full-cycle retry tests. Only two sequential backoff tests mutate it afterward, and the Go runner drains sequential tests before launching parallel ones, so it stays race-safe.
  - The orchestrator test fakes stay local and do not move to `go/test/fixtures`. These white-box tests exercise unexported internals, and `fixtures` imports core, which creates an import cycle.
  - `insertedLeakRunner` lives in the untagged `orchestrator_testfakes_test.go`. The untagged spine-gate test references it, and a definition behind `//go:build integration` does not compile there.
  - `captureStderr` redirects the process-global `os.Stderr`, so callers must not be parallel.
  - Real-repo fixtures follow production.
    - The repo's `.gitignore` ignores `.evolve/*`. The orchestrator's own workspace writes never show in `git status`, so the clean-tree assertion trips only on the leak under test.
    - The git identity lives in the repo and not in the helper's env. `RunCycle`'s own git children (the dossier-closeout commit) do not inherit it, and CI runners have no ambient identity.
  - `initInsertedLeakRepo` commits one source file first, so the leaked path is a new untracked file, the shape the guard's untracked-only baseline must catch. The leak write uses `Fatalf` and not `Errorf`, because a half-executed write must abort at once or the test reports a spurious guard RED.
  - The real-worktree provisioner's cleanup is a no-op on purpose. A shipped cycle's worktree must survive for the post-run `git diff HEAD`, and `t.TempDir` handles teardown. The runner executes synchronously on the test goroutine inside `RunCycle`, so a plain bool is race-free there.
  - Binding-cache pins stage a tracked modification and not an untracked file.
    - `worktreeContentSHA` stages with `git add -u`, so an untracked file keeps the base identity.
    - That lets the FAIL-verdict pin pass vacuously through the fresh-base guard.
    - The WARN-verdict positive control exists so that a red control marks the FAIL pin's no-cache assertion as untrustworthy.
  - The staging-scope pins require that residue (an unrelated untracked file) never changes a worktree's identity. `ProbeEligible` reads that identity to decide if a verdict can be cached or reused.
    - A declared new file must be retained.
    - A tracked edit that was never staged must be captured.
    - Otherwise the cheapest fixes (bind the base tree, stage nothing) look correct.
  - The phase-input tests rely on `reflect.DeepEqual` against a zero literal, because `PhaseInput` seals channels behind unexported fields. That is valid because `assemblePhaseIO` returns before any `NewPhaseInput` call below enforce.
  - The tier-elicitation tests read the shipped `agents/evolve-router.md` persona through `runtime.Caller`, so the path is cwd-independent and points at the worktree copy. The floor of two existing-phase examples is the anti-delete guard: deleting the persona's example, leaving only the Go-appended one, must not green the golden.
  - A failing Planner under `RunCycle` emits no orchestrator code naming the plan, because the advisor reports its own fault. The verbatim `[orchestrator] WARN phase advisor Plan failed` line still prints.
  - An unregistered signal code is rewritten to `SIGNALCENTER_UNREGISTERED_CODE` with the raw spelling under `fields.raw_code`.

## Invariants

- **The flow seats read their declared files (I-8, X8).** The plan judge, the retry adjudicator and the failure advisor resolve `routing-judge.json`, `failure-adjudication.json` and `failure-advice.json` through `phasecontract.FlowDecision.Resolve`. Each seat takes a Signal Center option (`WithPlanJudgeSignals`, `WithRetryAdjudicatorSignals`, `WithFailureAdvisorSignals`), and `cycleRun.reportProseFallbacks` emits each router-digest prose fallback once for each cycle. The guard `TestFlowCode_OnlyTheLegacySpanFileNamesLastBalancedSpan` keeps the stdout span in `advisor/span.go`. Matrix row N12 is green at `enforce`. Spec: [llm-isolation.md](../llm-isolation.md) §12.1.
- **A cycle number is never reused.** `allocateCycle` mints the lease before any run artifact exists, and crashed runs burn their numbers (`TestAllocateCycleNumber_ConcurrentAllocatorsDistinct`, `TestAllocateCycleNumber_CrashBurnsNumber`).
- **The cycle-end persist never rolls back the lease.** `persistCycleEndState` max-merges the lease and unions `FailedAt` and `CarryoverTodos` (`TestPersistCycleEndState_NeverRollsBackLease`, `TestMergeFailedRecords_UnionsDiskAndIncoming`, `TestMergeCarryoverTodos_DedupByID`).
- **Storage without `StateUpdater` behaves exactly as before the lease existed.** Pinned by `TestOrchestratorAllocateCycle_LegacyStorageFallsBack` and `TestPersistCycleEndState_LegacyStorageFallsBack`.
- **Deterministic evidence, then policy, then judgment.** The adjudicator chooses only among actions the envelope already allowed. It may be more conservative, never less, and it cannot overturn a floor halt. It is skipped when only one action is legal, so the deep tier is paid only where a real choice exists. A nil adjudicator (Null Object) still yields the policy default. Pinned by `TestDecideAfterAuditFail`, `TestDecideAfterAuditFail_AdjudicatorCannotOverturnTheFloor` and `TestDecideAfterAuditFail_NilAdjudicatorIsSafe`.
- **`audit→tdd` and `audit→build` are decision-only edges.**
  - They sit in the legality graph so `decideAfterAuditFail` can schedule them while `decisionOnlyEdge` blocks them from the routing advisor.
  - Advisor-routed re-entry skips `consumeAuditRepairGrant`, leaves retries uncounted up to `defaultMaxPhaseIterations` (32), or overrides `audit→ship`.
  - Pinned by `TestRouterCannotProposeTheAuditReentryEdges` and `TestAuditFailReentryEdgesAreLegal`.
- **`audit→audit` is a decision-only edge too.** It is the second audit after a passed `audit_fix` patch. Only `auditFixRound` schedules it, and `decisionOnlyEdge` refuses it to the routing advisor. Pinned by `TestRouterCannotProposeTheAuditReentryEdges`.
- **The adjudicator's reasoning reaches the operator.** `clampAdjudication` rejects an unjustified proposal because justification is the deliverable, and clamped proposals say so (`TestDecideAfterAuditFail_SurfacesTheAdjudicatorsReasoning`, `TestDecideAfterAuditFail_RecordsAClamp`).
- **`bridgeRetryAdjudicator` returns nil on failure paths, while `FailureAdvisor` returns an error.**
  - `clampAdjudication` turns nil into the policy default because the deterministic policy already fixed legal choices.
  - `adjudicationWire` stays separate from `adjudication` so wire changes cannot alter internal vocabulary.
  - `parseAdjudication` uses `lastBalancedSpan` rather than first-to-last braces so earlier objects do not break parsing.
- **The repair reason is built from `auditRepairReasonPrefix`, never a literal.** `TestAuditRepairReasonPrefix_IsSingleSourced` reads `audit_fail_decision.go` as text, so even a comment in that file must not spell the quoted literal `"audit-repair: "`.
- **Audit-round retirement ordering.**
  - `supersedePreviousAuditRound` runs before the pre-phase state write so crashed rounds retire dead pre-written verdicts on resume.
  - Round 0 retires nothing so pre-staged verdicts still work; existing archives are never overwritten, and rename errors remove stale files.
  - Pinned by `TestSupersedePreviousAuditRound_AdvancesThePersistedDispatchCounter`, `TestResumedCrashedAudit_RetiresTheDeadAttemptsVerdict`, `TestRetireSupersededAuditArtifacts_RoundZeroHonorsPreStagedVerdict`, `TestRetireSupersededAuditArtifacts_NeverClobbersAnExistingArchive`, `TestAuditRedispatch_RetiresPreviousRoundVerdicts` and `TestResumedAuditRedispatch_RetiresPreviousRoundVerdicts`.
- **The repair brief derives from persisted cycle state.**
  - Live and resume paths seed the same brief using the dispatched phase (`next`) without mutating caller `ctxSnap` maps.
  - `AuditRepairAttempts` is a monotonic counter, not an active flag (`TestSeedAuditRepairContext`, `TestSeedAuditRepairContext_DoesNotMutateCallerMap`, `TestAuditRepairBrief_SeededOnBothDispatchSurfaces`).
- **The audit repair consults the convergence policy after each audit FAIL, in shadow first** ([convergence-policy.md](../convergence-policy.md) section 9). `consultAuditRepairConvergence` builds the judgments from `audit-report.md.round<k>` and the live report, calls `convergence.Decide` for loop `audit-repair`, and emits `CONVERGENCE_RUNG`. The adapter keys a finding by its title, because report ids restart each round. It never defers or files a finding.
  - At the `shadow` stage the repair flow does not change. At the `enforce` stage only a `Stop` ends a repair that the retry envelope grants. A decision never lifts an envelope decline.
  - The adapter passes no fixer, judge or headroom yet. The judge raise and the fresh-context fixer reach the replay test only.
  - Pinned by `TestAuditRepairConvergence_ShadowRecordsTheRungAndLeavesTheFlowAlone`, `TestAuditRepairConvergence_EnforceStopEndsTheRepairTheEnvelopeWouldGrant` and `TestAuditRepairConvergence_EnforceNeverGrantsWhatTheEnvelopeDeclines`.
- **A gate-derived failure class comes from orchestrator memory and never outranks the auditor or regrade.**
  - `gateFailureBlock` reads only `CycleState.AuditFailReasons`; auditor blocks win, and regrade-only reasons stay apart from gate prefixes.
  - Identical gate FAILs follow the `code-audit-fail` budget (default 2; third declines as spent); ADR-0126 §10 stops repeats earlier.
  - Pinned by `TestAuditGateRemedies_EveryGateDeclaresARepairableClassAndARemediation`, `TestAuditGateFail_IdenticalGateFailsAreBoundedByTheEnvelopeBudget`, `TestAuditGateFail_BookkeepingConflictStillTakesTheRegrade` and `TestAuditGateFail_GateBesideBookkeepingEarnsNeitherRoute`.
- **The bookkeeping regrade cannot be triggered or unbounded by an agent.** Eligibility reads `CycleState.AuditFailReasons`, orchestrator memory set at the `recordFloorVerdictFailure` chokepoint, never a workspace file. The once-per-cycle bound is `CycleState.BookkeepingRegradeAttempted`, consumed by the one primitive `consumeBookkeepingRegradeGrant` that both branch surfaces call. The regrade sits below the ADR-0072 floor and above the router. Pinned by `TestDecideAfterRetro_RegradeOncePerCycle`, `TestDecideAfterRetroRouted_RegradeNotRouterOverridable`, `TestDecideAfterRetro_FloorOutranksRegrade` and `TestRecordAndBranch_RegradeGrantConsumesSlotAndSchedulesAudit`.
- **Unexplained digests never count as identical.** Distinct failures map to `|unknown|` or `Unexplained`, so the unexplained rule owns them; they still count toward consecutive streaks (`TestConsecutiveFailures_UnexplainedDigestsCountTowardTheStreak`).
- **The streak is keyed by cycle number.** Several digests from one cycle collapse to one entry, so a cycle that repaired twice cannot trip the ceiling-3 streak by itself. The audit-repair loop depends on this. Pinned by `TestBlockerBreaker_RepairAttemptsWithinOneCycleCountOnce` and `TestConsecutiveFailures_DuplicateDigestsForOneCycleCountOnce`.
- **The more specific rule names the halt.** `consecutive-failures` is evaluated last, so a guard-class or identical-fingerprint halt, whose reason carries an actionable repro hint, wins on overlap. Pinned by `TestConsecutiveFailures_GuardClassRuleWinsOnOverlap` and `TestConsecutiveFailures_IdenticalFingerprintRuleWinsOnOverlap`.
- **An ack is scoped to one fingerprint.** It excludes that fingerprint from Rule B and breaks streaks so verification runs do not halt (`TestEvaluateBlockerBreaker_UnackedIdenticalFingerprintStillHalts`, `TestConsecutiveFailures_AckCannotMaskAnIndependentlyTrippingHalf`).
- **Digest collection tolerates damage.** Missing or malformed `failure-digest.json` files are skipped so one corrupt artifact never disables the breaker (`TestCollectBatchFailureDigests_ScopesAndTolerates`).
- **The consumption parser fails closed.** It needs the `fingerprint` token so paths like `docs/a|b|c.md` never match (`TestParseConsumptionFingerprint_IgnoresUnrelatedPipeDelimitedText`, `TestConsumePipelineDefectFingerprint_ErrorsWhenNoFingerprintFound`).
- **Boot recovery never touches the loop's own state.** `.evolve/` and `knowledge-base/` churn is normal cycle activity and is never quarantined. `go/bin/evolve` is excluded twice: at classification, and again in the stash pathspec. Git may report a collapsed untracked parent directory (`go/`) that an exact-path exclusion cannot see. Because the same pass re-pins the ship binary, stashing it reverts to the old committed binary and reopens the SHA mismatch the re-pin heals. Pinned by `TestClassifyDirtyPaths_IgnoresLoopManaged`, `TestClassifyDirtyPaths_ExcludesShipBinary` and `TestQuarantineDirtyTree_LeavesStatusCleanAndPreservesContent`.
- **The build floor fails open on its own plumbing.** Nil engines, missing worktrees, `go list` errors, coverage errors, or apicover plumbing errors WARN and approve; test failures never drop (`TestBuildFloorReviewer_ChecksErrorFailsOpen`, `TestBuildTagVisiblePackagesFailsOpen`).
- **The floor diffs against the cycle base.** Builders commit work before review, so `HEAD` diffs read empty; missing bases fall back to `HEAD` plus untracked files (`TestDefaultBuildFloorChecks_SeesCommittedBuilderWork`).
- **The protected-surface floor runs with rename detection off.** `git diff --name-only` with renames hides old protected paths; nil predicates fail open (`TestProtectedSurfaceFloorChecks_SeesARenameOutOfTheSurface`, `TestProtectedSurfaceFloorChecks_RefusesTheCycle1689Shape`).
- **The repo-contract floor rejects only a named red.**
  - `packOutcome.realRed` splits named test/build failures from unnamed toolchain exits.
  - Named reds produce one floor line with failing tests, reproduction commands, and `floorFailureDiagnostic` tails.
  - Unnamed non-zero exits WARN with error, deadline, and output tail without blocking; `repoContractFloorDeadline` (120 s) bounds the pack.
  - The pack keeps `-timeout 20m` internally; green runs, missing worktrees, and `off` dials stay silent.
  - Pinned by `TestRepoContractFloorChecks_ARawGitFixtureIsCorrectedAtBuildExitByTheRatchetsName`, `TestRepoContractFloorChecks_GreenOrAnUnnamedExitPassesTheHandoff`, `TestRepoContractFloorChecks_AnyNamedRedCorrectsTheBuild`, `TestRepoContractFloorChecks_RunsThePackUnderItsOwnDeadline` and `TestRepoContractFloorChecks_ADeadlineKillWarnsAndPassesTheHandoff`.
  - Pinned also by `TestRepoContractFloorChecks_FollowsTheShipGatesDial`, `TestRepoContractFloorChecks_WithoutAProjectRootTheWorktreesPolicyDecides`, `TestRepoContractPack_ShipAndTheBuildFloorMakeOneDecision` and `TestRepoContractFloor_ARedPackCorrectsTheBuildAndNeverReachesTheAudit`.
- **The apicover floor is diff-scoped.**
  - Touched-file violations hard-fail while older package debt WARNs; a coverage profile prevents false-green exports.
  - The coverage run takes `verifylock` and degrades to unserialized runs on lock contention instead of skipping.
  - When it cannot take the lock, `ReviewInput.recordUnserializedFloor` writes `verify-single-flight-unavailable` (`TestScopedCoverFunc_ALostSingleFlightIsAnAbnormalEventNamingTheLockPath`, [internal-verifylock](internal-verifylock.md)).
- **Packages with no Go files under the default tags are dropped before the selfcheck.** `go test` on one is a setup failure ("build constraints exclude all Go files"), not a test failure. This is the second line of defense; the first is that modern `acs` packages are not enrolled in `.apicover-enforce` (see its rationale block). Pinned by `TestBuildTagVisiblePackagesDropsACSPackages` and `TestAPICoverEnforceDoesNotEnrollModernACSPackages`.
- **A failing package's diagnostic keeps the last 400 bytes.** `go test` writes its `--- FAIL` lines, panics and stack traces at the end, and this text is the whole story the next builder is handed. Complete output carries no elision marker, so the marker stays a trustworthy signal. Pinned by `TestBuildFloorSelfCheckFailures_KeepsTailDiagnostic`.
- **`docsFloorWarn` is read as text by an ACS predicate.** `go/acs/cycle1151` counts the lines of that function's body (`acsassert.CountInGoFunc`, comments included) that contain `docsfloor.IsArchitectureClass(` or `docsfloor.LabelArchitecture(`, and requires exactly one labeler. A comment inside `docsFloorWarn` must never spell either call with its opening parenthesis.
- **A menu phase must resolve a persona from `agents/<agent>.md`.** That is the only persona source the dispatch path reads for a disk-loaded spec (`prompts.Loader.Agent` is single-rooted; specrunner's inline `PromptBody` is mint-only). A phase-local `agent.md` is a scaffold for humans and does not count. Control-role detection uses the production inference (`RoleOrDefault`), never a private allowlist. Pinned by `TestRepoPhaseCatalog_MenuPhasesResolveAPersona`.
- **A missing persona skips only a genuinely optional phase.** Mandatory phases, ship-floor phases (even one mis-marked Optional) and configured-mandatory phases still fail loudly, so the skip can never weaken ship ⇒ build ∧ audit ∧ tdd. The skip is ledgered under its own kind, `optional_missing_persona_skip`, never the infra key, and is learned deterministically without dispatching a retrospective agent. Pinned by the tests in `agent_doc_missing_test.go`.
- **`catalog: "on-demand"` hides a phase from the SELECT menu without removing it.**
  - The phase stays dispatchable by plan and mintable while listed in one declined-phase index line; unknown words fail catalog validation.
  - Every render layer is pinned (`TestAdvisorPrompt_OnDemandPhaseLeavesTheMenuButStaysNamed`, `TestComposePlanPrompt_CarriesTheOnDemandIndex`, `TestBuildPlanPrompt_CarriesTheOnDemandIndex`, `TestAdvisorPlanInput_PopulatesOnDemandFromTheOrchestratorCatalog`, `TestRepoPhaseCatalog_CatalogWordIsKnown`, `TestKnownCatalogWord_RejectsTypos`).
- **The shipped registry's activating fields agree with the literal kernel.** ADR-0058 made the kernel config-driven with a literal backstop so dropped fields cannot revert behavior silently. `builtinControlSpec` for the debugger must match the literal too (`TestRegistryActivatingFields_AgreeWithLiteralKernel`, `TestControlSeamActivatingFields_AgreeWithLiteralKernel`).
- **`PhaseBoundaryCheckpointer` is package-global.** The checkpoint package sets it from `init()`, and core cannot import that package. Tests assign their own closure and must run sequentially instead of in parallel with other `RunCycle` tests.
- **A removal claim is judged against the repository, and only claims can fail.**
  - Missing-on-disk tracked files fail; untracked absent files, non-repos, escaping paths, and bad JSON pass or fail open.
  - Claims failing on both axes report once with the worktree message (`TestRemovalClaimFailures*`, `TestDefaultBuildFloorChecks_IncludesRemovalClaimCheck`, `TestBuildFloorReviewer_RemovalClaimNotActuallyRemoved`).
- **Only an `UPPER_SNAKE` token ending in `_PLACEHOLDER` is a slot**; prose, lowercase names and a missing report pass. Pinned by `TestPlaceholderTokenFailures_RefusesAnUnsubstitutedTemplateToken`, `TestPlaceholderTokenFailures_CleanReportAndProseAreNotTokens` and `TestDefaultBuildFloorChecks_IncludesThePlaceholderTokenCheck`.
- **A token quoted inside a `## Correction N` section is exempt from the placeholder check; a bare token is not.** The correction prompt (`correctionRecord` in `retry_backoff.go`) makes the builder quote the finding it fixed, so the check used to reject the repaired report. A heading alone never hides a slot, and only a backtick span or a fenced block is skipped. The section opens at `## Correction <digits>`, titled forms included, and ends at the next `# ` or `## ` heading, while a `### ` subheading keeps it open. `## Corrections` and every later section are checked in full. Pinned by `TestPlaceholderTokenFailures_BareTokenUnderCorrectionHeadingFails`, `TestPlaceholderTokenFailures_QuotedTokenUnderCorrectionHeadingPasses`, `TestPlaceholderTokenFailures_SubheadingKeepsCorrectionSectionOpen`, `TestPlaceholderTokenFailures_H1EndsCorrectionSection` and `TestPlaceholderTokenFailures_TitledCorrectionHeadingsOpenASection`.
- **A `# ` or `## ` heading closes a fence left open inside a correction section.** An unclosed fence therefore cannot exempt the sections after it. Pinned by `TestPlaceholderTokenFailures_UnclosedFenceInCorrectionEndsAtTheNextHeading` and `TestPlaceholderTokenFailures_FenceClosesAndBareTokenAfterItFails`.
- **The persona budget check is a conjunction.** A touched persona doc alone never rejects, and a lane that touches no persona doc never runs the package. Pinned by `TestPersonaBudgetFailures` and `TestDefaultBuildFloorChecks_IncludesPersonaBudgetCheck`.
- **Build-leak recovery preserves work and never widens the audit view.**
  - Staging uses relocated paths only, never `git add -A`, which sweeps worktree leftovers into the audit diff.
  - Tracked leaks relocate only when the worktree copy matches HEAD (`M ` staged case included); diverged worktrees win and discard main leaks.
  - `.gitignore` discards build outputs and `go/evolve` is always discarded; `.evolve/` runtime state and nested worktrees skip, while deliverables relocate unless foreign-owned.
  - Pre-phase operator dirt stays untouched; correction re-dispatches recover leaks before review (`TestRecoverBuildLeak_*`, `TestCorrectionRedispatch_RecoversLeakBeforeReview`).
- **The carry-forward screen separates conflict from supersession.** A real conflict is `(false, nil)`, never an error; only a git failure errors. Supersession runs before the merge dry run, because a dry run of an already-absorbed change reads clean. A not-landable candidate is `FleetRebaseAlreadyLanded` only when the supersession screen says so, since calling a conflict landed drops overlapping work. Pinned by `TestCarryforwardCandidateLandable_ConflictIsFalseNotError`, `TestCarryforwardCandidateLandable_SupersededShortCircuitsBeforeMerge`, `TestCarryforwardCandidateLandable_PatchIdDuplicateSuperseded`, `TestClassifyFleetRebaseCandidate_ThreeWayVerdicts` and `TestClassifyFleetRebaseCandidate_GitInfraErrorIsError`.
- **The composition fast paths can only narrow what ships.** A missing seam, patch-id drift, a red composed-tree gate, a reviewer error or a writer error falls back to the full re-audit. A RUNG 2 `compatible` verdict composes only when its resolution re-verifies by patch-id against the audited change, never on the reviewer's word. `Composes` is true only for `compatible`, so an unknown verdict fails closed. "Wired" means all three RUNG 0 closures are bound. Pinned by `TestOrchestrator_CompositionFastPathWired`, `TestScopedReviewVerdict_Composes`, `TestReverifyResolution_MatchAndReject`, the `TestIntersectingHunks_*` tests and `mergerung2_consolidation_test.go`.
- **Carryover retirement and minting are conservative.**
  - `RetireCarryoverTodos` removes committed ids and fingerprint twins in order without mutating input or matching blank ids.
  - Defect todos inherit `ExpiresAt` or stay unstamped; cross-cycle re-mints collapse and refresh expiry; closeout merges memos, prescriptions, then triage retirements.
  - Pinned by `TestRetireCarryoverTodos_*`, `TestApplyDefectsAsCarryoverTodos_StampsExpiryFromRecord`, `TestApplyDefectsAsCarryoverTodos_NoRecordExpiryLeavesTodoUnstamped`, `TestAdoptDefects_CrossCycleFingerprintDedupe`, `TestAdoptDefects_SuppressedRemintRefreshesExpiry`, `TestFinalizeCycle_CloseoutKeepsTheThreeStepOrder` and `TestFinalizeCycle_RetiresTriageDroppedCarryover`.
- **Every carryover construction lives in `carryover_lifecycle.go`.** `TestCarryoverLifecycle_OneConstructionSite` scans non-test Go files for `carryover.New(`, `MergeWorkspaceCarryover(`, `MergeWorkspacePrescriptionCarryover(` and `ApplyDefectsAsCarryoverTodos(`.
- **A console lease waives only matching paths.** Absent, malformed, or expired leases yield empty sets; checkout-local leases are ignored, and waivers warn (`TestReadConsoleLease_*`, `TestFilterRealLeaks_LeaseWaiverIsExactAndLoud`).
- **A continuation binds only resumable, immutable work.**
  - Snapshots commit to branch history and stamp manifests only when clean against main and scoped to non-empty lane ids.
  - Unrelated scopes never adopt, and registry errors WARN without failing finalization.
  - Pinned by `TestSnapshotPreservedWorktree_CommitsDirtAndUntracked`, `TestStampContinuationManifest_WritesGatedManifest`, `TestStampContinuationManifest_ConflictingWorkIsNotStamped`, `TestStampContinuationManifest_UnstampableWorkRegistersNothing`, `TestStampContinuationManifest_NoLaneScopeRegistersNothing`, `TestStampContinuationManifest_RegistersEveryLaneScopeID` and `TestRunCycle_UnrelatedLaneScopeDoesNotAdopt`.
- **A rejected adoption sheds the pointer on the item.** After `releaseDeclinedBinding` deletes the registry entries, `shedDeclinedPointer` calls the injected `WithContinuationShedder` port, so the claimed item loses its `continuation` block in the same reject step. The core cannot import `inboxmover` (an import cycle), so `cmd/evolve` injects the port. A nil port sheds nothing, and a port fault is a WARN. Pinned by `TestRunCycle_ARejectedAdoptionLeavesNoContinuationOnTheItem` and `TestShedDeclinedPointer_AShedderFaultIsAWarning`.
- **A fresh cycle settles the registry binding after the triage adoption.** `adoptAndSettleAfterTriage` calls the injected `WithBindingSettler` port when adoption returns no error. The port releases the binding or records it in the manifest, the same step as a lane resume. When the port records a binding, `settleBinding` seeds the disposition skeleton and serves the inherited findings to the builder.

  A nil port settles nothing. The core cannot import `inboxmover`, so `cmd/evolve` injects the port. Pinned by `TestRunCycle_FreshCycleSettlesRegistryBindingAfterTriage` and `TestRunCycle_RecordedSettlementServesTheInheritedDebtToTheBuilder`.
- **The abnormal epilogue stamps a continuation too.** It runs after the failure digest, so the manifest's `FindingsPath` names the `audit-fail-reason.json` reason artifact rather than the digest. Pinned by `TestAbnormalEpilogue_StampsContinuation` and `TestStampContinuationManifest_WritesGatedManifest`.
- **The console adopts a continuation through the lane code** (`AdoptContinuationForConsole`, `continuation_console_adopt.go`, 2026-10-27).
  - It takes one `ConsoleAdoptRequest`. The warnings of the findings go to the `Warn` writer of the caller, and a nil `Warn` discards them. The base-advance line of the lane code still goes to stderr. A relative worktree path is under the project root.
  - It runs `validateContinuation`, adds the worktree on the branch `adopt/<item-id>` from the snapshot, and runs `advanceContinuationBase`.
  - It writes `<worktree>.findings.md` from `readContinuationFindings` and `continuationRepairFirst`, outside the worktree, so the worktree stays clean.
  - `validateContinuation` returns `ErrContinuationConflict` only for the Conflict verdict, so another failed check keeps its own cause. On that sentinel the console adds the conflicting files from `git merge-tree --name-only` (`snapshotConflicts`).
  - A fault after the worktree add removes the worktree and deletes the branch (`undoConsoleWorktree`), so a second try does not meet a stale branch.
  - Pinned by the `TestAdoptContinuationForConsole_*` tests and `TestUndoConsoleWorktree_NamesEachStepThatFails`.
- **A failed base advance stops before Build.**
  - Conflicting merges abort under `context.WithoutCancel` so cancellation never leaves half-merged work; rejected snapshots trigger no phase and lose no work.
  - Pinned by `TestAdvanceContinuationBase_ConflictReturnsErrorAndAborts`, `TestAdvanceContinuationBase_NoOpWhenCurrent` and `TestRunCycle_ContinuationAdvanceFailureStopsBeforeBuild` (integration tag).
- **Releasing a declined binding removes only the declined scopes.** A scope bound to a different ancestor survives. Pinned by `TestReleaseDeclinedBinding_RemovesMatchingScopesOnly`.
- **An exhausted dispatch loop is an explained, cycle-level failure.**
  - When the iteration bound ends the loop before `PhaseEnd`, it records a terminal abort naming the transition-table cycle.
  - `cyclehealth.ClassifyOutcome` then reads `FAILED_EXPLAINED` and `RunCycle` returns no batch-fatal error (`TestRunCycle_TransitionCycleGuard_RecordsChokepointEscape`).
- **A pre-handoff probe runs the cycle's floor list with the cycle's input.**
  - `BuildHandoffFloorNames()` equals the probes' floor names, listing mounted floors; both probes run each floor once on `ReviewInputFor` of the bound cycle.
  - Resumed legacy checkpoints review under the resumed cycle (`TestResumeBootstrap_LegacyCheckpointReviewsUnderTheResumedCycle`).
  - Pinned by `TestOrchestrator_BuildHandoffFloorNames_ListsEveryFloorCheckTheReviewerRuns`, `TestBuildHandoffProbes_IterateTheCycleFloorsCheckList` and `TestBuildHandoffProbes_BothRunTheSeamFloorOnTheBoundCyclesInput`.
- **`ChangedWorktreePaths` reports tracked and untracked changes and is empty for a clean repo or non-repo.** That makes the docs floor skip instead of inventing violations. Pinned by `TestChangedWorktreePaths_ExportedForCLIConsumers` and `TestChangedWorktreePaths_EmptyWorktreeIsEmpty`.
- **A trailing `WithCatalogPublisher(nil)` never unwires an earlier publisher.** Pinned by `TestWithCatalogPublisher_TrailingNilDoesNotUnwireAnEarlierValidPublisher`. A nil catalog refresher writes no `catalog_refresh` ledger entry, because a refresher that never ran has no outcome to stamp. Pinned by `TestOrchestrator_CatalogRefresh_NilRefresherNoLedgerEntry`.
- **Source readers of these files.**
  - `go/acs/cycle1153` counts quoted `"build-report.md"` and `"audit-report.md"` literals in `readBuildReport`, comments included.
  - `go/acs/cycle1152` requires `build_removal_check.go` to call `phasecontract.ArtifactName(` or `phasecontract.ArtifactFilename(`.
  - `go/acs/cycle968` requires `ClassifyFleetRebaseCandidate` to mention `CarryforwardCandidateLandable`.
  - `go/acs/cycle1159` counts non-test files outside `carryforward_filter.go` mentioning `ClassifyFleetRebaseCandidate` as callers, so comments elsewhere fake one.
  - `go/acs/cycle1181` requires `carryforward_filter.go` to carry no `QUARANTINED-DEAD` marker.
- **A bridge or phase error is cycle-level; only kernel-integrity invariants stay batch-fatal.**
  - `RunCycle` wraps bridge exhaustion in `ErrCycleLevelFailure` (`Unwrap` preserves root causes), while phase-gate denials, broken ledgers, and lock contention stay unwrapped.
  - The audit↔ship recovery loop allows one first try plus `maxRecoveryDepth` (2) re-audits (same bound for ship), and exhaustion is cycle-level too.
  - Pinned by `TestErrCycleLevelFailure_WrapsCauseForErrorsIs`, `TestOrchestrator_BridgeExhaustion_CycleLevelFailure`, `TestOrchestrator_IntegrityBreach_StillBatchFatal` and `TestOrchestrator_RecoveryDepthBudget`.
- **A salvage snapshot never commits the project root.** When the worktree resolves to the project root (including symlinks or relative paths), stamping refuses loudly. In-place work stays out of snapshots. Pinned by `TestStampContinuation_NeverSnapshotsTheProjectRoot`.
- **A teardown-shaped abort fingerprint keeps its population membership.**
  - `IsInfraTeardownError` causes mark `teardown=`, never `cause=`, and stay in Rule B.
  - Systemic infra failures hitting several lanes must trip ADR-0072's ceiling; the marker clarifies the halt message without exempting it (`TestAbnormalEpilogue_TeardownMarkedAndTailKept`).
- **`clearActiveWorktree` only unnames the worktree it just pruned.**
  - It reads the state from storage, changes it and writes it back. It does not write back the `newCycleRun`-era snapshot.
  - Teardown runs after the cycle saves phase progress in its `CycleState`, so writing an old snapshot deletes progress.
  - The path guard makes it a no-op once the record changes.
- **The cross-artifact invariant stack binds to the LANE worktree, never `projectRoot`.**
  - In fleet mode a project-root snapshot names another lane's tree, so `finalizeCycle` runs `coherence.CheckCrossArtifactInvariants` over `cs.ActiveWorktree`.
  - Findings stay advisory, change no verdict, raise no system failure, and record on every cycle to measure false-positive rates before blocking.
  - Pinned by `TestFinalizeCycle_EmitsAdvisoryCrossArtifactInvariantsArtifact`, `TestFinalizeCycle_CrossArtifactViolationsNeverBlockTheCycle`, `TestFinalizeCycle_CrossArtifactBindsTheLaneWorktreeNotTheProjectRoot` and `TestFinalizeCycle_VerdictIncoherenceFloorSurvivesTheAdvisory`.
- **The deliverable-kind digest degrades loud, defaults quiet.** A read failure or a `DigestDegraded` reason WARNs on stderr and keeps the conservative `code` kind; a clean absence (no report yet) is silent. A `.evolve/domain.json` that exists but fails to parse is reported and counts as absent, never a silent reclassification. Pinned by `TestDispatchSignals`, `TestDispatchSignals_DegradedDigestIsLoud` and `TestResolveDeliverableKind_DeclaredBeatsTheDomainDefaultWhichBeatsCode`.
- **The worktree fence is one value from one projection** (ADR-0097; one value since 2026-10-21, inbox `worktree-fence-one-value`).
  - `PhaseRequest.Fence` is a `*WorktreeFence`. A nil fence means that the phase writes source. A fence means read-only, except the paths in `Fence.Writable`. Thus a request cannot carry writable paths without a fence.
  - `FenceFor(writesSource, writable)` is the one projection. `PhaseWritesSource(phase, catalog)` is the one rule: the builtin source writers, or a catalog spec with `writes_source`.
  - The orchestrator calls `o.fenceFor(phase, cs)` in the live, resume and evaluate-batch requests, the out-of-band retro and a remediation fix. `ReauditCycle`, `evolve campaign`, `evolve phase --cycle` and `evolve compose --cycle` call `FenceFor`.
  - The structure test `TestEveryPhaseRequestWithAWorktreeTakesTheFenceFromTheOneProjection` enumerates every `PhaseRequest` literal that sets a worktree. Each one must take `Fence` from `FenceFor` or `fenceFor`.
  - The guard reads literals only. A later assignment to `Fence` is not scanned; today the only one is the remediation fix, which calls `fenceFor`. `evolve compose --cycle` runs several phases on one request, so the request is unfenced when any of them writes source.
  - The wire carries both spellings of the fence: `worktree_fence` and the legacy keys `worktree_read_only` and `worktree_writable_paths`. A request decoded with only the legacy keys gets its fence back, so a phase binary on either side of the rename stays fenced.
  - `ReauditCycle` runs its audit behind the read-only fence (`TestReauditCycle_TheAuditRunsBehindTheReadOnlyFence`).
  - `PhaseRequest.WritesSource()` is true for a nil fence or for writable paths. The phase runner hands it to the skill-overlay resolver. `BridgeCompletion` asks only if a fence exists, so a conflict-resolving debugger gets no worktree-evidence contract.
  - Pinned by `TestFenceFor_IsTheOneProjection`, `TestOutOfBandRetro_IsFencedLikeEveryReadOnlyPhase`, `TestOutOfBandRetro_AWritingRetroOfAFailedBuildIsHeldByTheFence`, `TestPhaseRequest_WritesSource_TheWorktreeFenceDecidesWhoWrites` and `TestDispatch_ReadOnlyPhasesAreFencedAndSourceWritersAreNot`.
- **The spine gate blocks only a clean absence at enforce.** A degraded digest or a re-digest error is never treated as a clean absence, so it always fails open, even at enforce. A transient read fault must never false-block a real cycle. A config-waived anchor (removed from `cfg.Mandatory`) cannot block either. Pinned by `TestSpineGate_EnforceBlocksOnCleanAbsence`, `TestSpineGate_ShadowKeepsFailOpen`, `TestSpineGate_EnforceFailsOpenOnDegradedDigest`, `TestSpineGate_ConfigWaiverSkipsAnchor` and `TestDigest_DegradedVsAbsent`. The gate keys on its own `SpineFloor` dial, not `PhaseRecovery`, because `PhaseRecovery` is overloaded (bidirectional channel plus failure-adviser promotion) and stays at shadow.
- **A preserved worktree keeps its `ActiveWorktree` path.** `evolve loop --resume` and `evolve cycle reset` reclaim preserved lanes by that path. Clearing it unconditionally trades a stale-path bug for orphaned audited work (`TestCycleRunTeardown_PreservedWorktreeKeepsActiveWorktree`, `TestCycleRunTeardown_ClearsActiveWorktreeAfterPrune`).
- **The debugger gate consults the catalog, not a literal `current == PhaseDebugger` check.**
  - A registry override to verdict branching disables the signal branch (no successor, no loopBreak on BLOCK); without one, the seam supplies signal.
  - `sm.Next` stays on the literal debugger→end sentinel because the seam declares no verdict branches.
  - Pinned by `TestSuccessorStrategy_Debugger`, `TestBuiltinControlSpec`, `TestRecordAndBranch_DebuggerGateIsStrategyKeyed`, `TestRecordAndBranch_DebuggerDegradesToSignalViaSeam` and `TestNext_DebuggerSeamDoesNotLeakIntoVerdictBranch`.

- **A wall on a correction, a remediation re-run or a resumed review is a deferral; any other dispatch failure there is still a failure.** Pinned by `TestRunCycle_WalledCorrectionRedispatch_DefersLikeAFirstDispatch`, `TestRunCycle_WalledRemediationRerun_DefersLikeAFirstDispatch`, `TestReviewResumedDeliverable_WalledCorrection_ReturnsTheExhaustionSentinel`, `TestRunCycleFromPhase_WalledResumedCorrection_Defers` and `TestRunCycle_FailedCorrectionRedispatch_IsNotADeferral`.

- **An explanation-review advisory raised on a PASS verdict rides the C1 phase-outcome record** ([ADR-0102](../adr/0102-explanation-review-reasoning-is-the-gate.md)).
  - That persistence supports the feedback loop, while `internal/phases/audit` handles emitting the prefixed advisory on PASS (`TestRecordPhaseOutcome_AnAdvisoryOnAPassRidesTheRecord`).
- **A missing `## Explanation Documentation` section in an audit report is a correction, not a terminal FAIL.**
  - Deliverable-reviewer rejection re-dispatches the audit runner once with the reason as directive, matching other contract-rejection ladders (`TestAuditContractRejection_RedispatchesTheAuditorWithTheReason`).
- **`enforceNext` skips must never rewrite `staticNext` to `PhaseEnd` when `cfg.Order` is empty.**
  - Without `phase-registry.json`, `nextInOrder` returns `PhaseEnd` for absent phases and can stop cycles after `tdd`. Keeping `staticNext` avoids that (`TestEnforceNext_EmptyOrderSkipAdvance`).
- **`decideAfterRetro` calls `failureadapter.Decide` with `Strict=false`.**
  - Every non-Proceed `Decision` gates behind `if opts.Strict`, so `Decide` only returns `ActionProceed` from this call site.
  - Setting `Strict=true` makes RETRY and BLOCK reachable, requiring new tests (`TestDecideAfterRetro`).
- **`NewPhaseAdvisor`'s default identity is `claude-tmux`/`opus`, and empty overrides never blank it.**
  - Model defaults to `opus` for deep cycle composition; the composition root overrides from the router profile plus `EVOLVE_ROUTER_CLI` and `EVOLVE_ROUTER_MODEL` (`TestPhaseAdvisorOptions`).
- **A sealed FAIL always carries at least one reason.**
  - `backfillFailReasons` fills `CycleResult.FailReasons` when empty so the breaker sees a real fingerprint.
  - Priority: abort reasons, failing phases' error diagnostics, failing phase names with `phase-infra class`, or an unexplained marker (never null).
  - Explained FAILs (audit/ship reasons present) and non-FAIL verdicts stay untouched.
  - Pinned by `TestBackfillFailReasons_FromAbortReasons`, `TestBackfillFailReasons_UnexplainedMarkerNeverNull`, `TestBackfillFailReasons_NoOpWhenExplainedOrNotFAIL` and `TestBackfillFailReasons_PhaseOwnDiagnosticsNameTheReason`.
- **`FailureAdvisor` is the deterministic-first, LLM-last tail for `CauseUnknown`** ([ADR-0044](../adr/0044-unified-phase-recovery-protocol.md)).
  - Built like `PhaseAdvisor` (bridge-dispatched, strict-JSON-parsed), every failure (nil bridge, error, bad output, unknown cause, non-fatal) errors so callers escalate safely.
  - `recovery.PromoteAdvice` promotes verdicts into the deterministic registry, avoiding hot-loop LLM calls.
  - Prompts carry `# Usage evidence` from the last five records of `usage-evidence.ndjson` (framed untrusted, see [internal-usageevidence](internal-usageevidence.md)) before reading panes.
  - Pinned by `TestFailureAdvisor_PromptCarriesTheUsageEvidenceRecordedForTheFailure`, `TestFailureAdvisor_ParsesCauseAndSignature`, `TestFailureAdvisor_EmptyCause_NonFatalSignal`, `TestFailureAdvisor_MalformedJSON_ReturnsError`, `TestFailureAdvisor_UnknownCause_ReturnsError`, `TestFailureAdvisor_NilBridge_FailSafe` and `TestFailureAdvisor_BridgeError_Propagates`.
- **`readFailureDecision` is the fail-soft boundary for `failure-decision.json`** ([ADR-0072](../adr/0072-system-failure-policy-and-halt.md)).
  - Absent, malformed, or invalid artifacts return `(nil, nil)` to fall back to `failureadapter`, avoiding garbled branches.
  - Kept unexported to avoid apicover surface; it round-trips the retrospective persona schema (`agents/evolve-retrospective.md` tested by `C1002_005`).
  - Pinned by `TestReadFailureDecision` and `TestFailureDecisionWiring`.
- **The `FailureAdvisor` prompt is the one shipped LLM reader of raw pane text, framed through `panetrust.Frame`** ([ADR-0045](../adr/0045-corrective-interaction-protocol.md) I5). It adds an untrusted preamble, a neutralized fence, and redacted secrets (`TestFailureAdvisor_PromptCarriesUntrustedFraming`).
- **`FailureDigest` deterministically builds a stable failure identity before retro so retro cannot invent one.**
  - `AssembleFailureDigest` reads `audit-fail-reason.json` fail-soft: absent or invalid files degrade to `"unknown"` and write a digest; only write errors fail.
  - Pre-class buckets match lowercased reason text against an ordered table, checking infra teardown before gate buckets.
  - Pinned by `TestAssembler_PreClassBucketsFromRealArtifacts`, `TestAssembler_FingerprintComposition`, `TestAssembler_RecurrenceFromLedger`, `TestAssembler_MissingArtifactsDegradeToUnknown` and `TestAssembler_WritesDigestArtifact`.
- **Fingerprint normalization strips only per-cycle tokens for one defect, keeping defect-distinguishing content.**
  - It strips `narrative=<verdict>`, go-test decimal durations (keeping integer config like `-timeout 300s`), cycle paths, `"cycle N"`, and try denominators.
  - Narrow anchors keep distinct defects separate.
  - Pinned by `TestNormalizeReasonForFingerprint_CycleNumberedPathsFold`, `TestNormalizeReasonForFingerprint_AttemptDenominatorFolds`, `TestNormalizeReasonForFingerprint_DistinctDefectsStayDistinct`, `TestNormalizeReasonForFingerprint_ExistingPinsStayGreen` and `TestFailureDigest_DurationTokensNormalizeOut`.
- **`verdictFailDistinguisher` layers by strength: defect identity first, task identity last.**
  - A sentinel defect list counts only when the sentinel carries `failure.class`; classless sentinels fall back to report bullets.
  - Task identity is weak (task retries hit new defects, and repeats belong to S5 quarantine), so it runs only without defects.
  - Pinned by `TestVerdictFailDistinguisher_SentinelDefectsOutrankTaskIDs`, `TestVerdictFailDistinguisher_FallsBackTasksWhenNoDefects`, `TestVerdictFailDistinguisher_ClasslessSentinelFallsSoftToBullets` and `TestFailureDigest_SameTaskDistinctDefectsGetDistinctFingerprints`.
- **`defectHead` truncates from the head, `causeHead` from the tail.**
  - Error chains grow prefix-first (`"phase build: attempt N: bridge: <long path>: <root cause>"`), so keeping the tail preserves root causes.
  - Defect lines carry their identity at the head (`TestAbnormalEpilogue_TeardownMarkedAndTailKept`).
- **`ensureFailureDigest` is shared by both retro dispatch paths (`recordFailureLearning` error path and `cyclerun` verdict-FAIL path).**
  - Wiring only one left verdict FAILs without digest or disposition. It is best-effort: nil ledgers yield recurrence 0 and write failures warn.
  - Fallback reasons write `audit-fail-reason.json` only when floors wrote none, so floors always win.
  - Agent-graded build-repair deaths digest their own failure; runner-downgraded repair deaths digest the gate record ([logic-first design §5.14](../logic-first-delivery-design.md)).
  - Pinned by `TestEnsureFailureDigest_WritesDigestWithoutLedger`, `TestEnsureFailureDigest_Idempotent`, `TestEnsureFailureDigest_MissingWorkspaceNeverWritesToWorkingDirectory` and `TestRunCycle_AReauthorBuildThatDiesIsDigestedFromItsOwnFailure`.
- **`buildFailureDossier` composes independent evidence instead of trusting only the recorded verdict.**
  - It combines verdict-coherence signals, self-declared failure envelopes (surfaced even for task classes), and non-progress counters.
  - `FloorCandidate` sets only for the two deterministic floor categories; structural system audit classes trigger it, while prose claims reach judgment.
  - `SubstantiveError` matches `system_failure.go` by folding `cs.ShipFailReasons` into `"coherent"` so both sites agree (`TestFailureDossier`).
- **The build's system class defers the item; it never halts the loop** (`builderSystemClassUnderGates`, `Orchestrator.failureDossier`, the cycle-1841 shape). The auditor wrote PASS and declared no failure block, so only deterministic gates forced the FAIL (a `verdict-conflict` record). The build writes `build-report.md`, so its word alone must not stop the batch ([ADR-0072](../adr/0072-system-failure-policy-and-halt.md), "a builder's system class defers, never halts").
  - The claim counts only with orchestrator corroboration. At least one repair round ran. The last two audits of the run bound the same worktree tree (`auditedTreeUnchanged`, the auditor ledger rows). An unchanged tree means that the remedy round moved nothing.
  - The build must hand off WARN or FAIL with a system-level class. The class is normalized to its policy category, for example `infrastructure-systemic` to `infra-systemic`, and it is recorded as `build_declared`. It never sets the floor candidate.
  - `decideAfterAuditFail` then declines a further `code-audit-fail` round. Retro ends the cycle through the defer rule. `deferDecision` takes `build_declared` from orchestrator memory after the audit block and before `failure-decision.json`. The fix type is `pipeline-repair`. The closeout routes the item to wait on a console `pipeline-repair-cycle-<N>` item. A false claim thus costs one lane, not the batch.
  - These shapes keep today's dossier and today's repair decision:
    - a builder WARN with no class or with a task-level class;
    - a FAIL narrative of the auditor;
    - a claim before the remedy round, a PASS build or a changed tree.
  - Pinned by `TestDecideAfterAuditFail_ABuildersSystemClassNeverHaltsTheLoop` (an unchanged skills-drift or gofmt reading), `TestDecideAfterAuditFail_AChangedAuditedTreeKeepsTheRepairRound`, `TestAuditedTreeUnchanged_ComparesTheLastTwoAuditsOfTheRun`, `TestFailureDossier_TodaysDossierHoldsWithoutTheShape`, `TestFailureDossier_ANonFloorSystemClassIsRecordedAndIsNoFloor` and `TestAuditDeclineDefers_APlantedReadOnlyDossierClaimingASystemClassDoesNotDefer`.
- **A cycle's `FinalVerdict` is floor-gated: only an authoritative phase sets it directly once one exists.**
  - `isAuthoritativePhase` covers the ship floor plus ship; `floorAlreadyCompleted` checks `cs.CompletedPhases` so resume shares the guard.
  - Post-floor non-floor outcomes record in `CycleResult.VerdictsNotAdopted` instead of overwriting the floor verdict (`nonFloorExhaustionDegrade` degrades to SKIPPED+WARN).
  - Pre-floor phase failures stay cycle-fatal (`TestNonFloorPhaseFailure_DoesNotOverrideFloorVerdict`, `TestNonFloorPhaseFailure_FailAudit_StaysFail`, `TestFloorPhaseFailure_RemainsCycleFatal`, `TestResumeNonFloorPhaseFailure_DoesNotOverrideFloorVerdict`, `TestContractExhaustion_NonFloorPhase_DegradesToSkippedWarn`).
- **A floor phase's success-path FAIL feeds failure-learning through the same core as an error-path failure.**
  - `floorVerdictError` joins error-severity diagnostics; `recordFailedApproachState` appends `FailedRecord`, dedupes P0 carryover, and stamps `LastCycleNumber`.
  - `recordAndBranch` triggers only for the default floor on FAIL and persists directly (`TestFloorVerdictError_JoinsErrorSeverityDiagnostics`, `TestRecordFailedApproachState_RecordsFloorFailToStateAndCarryover`, `TestRecordFailedApproachState_DedupesCarryover`, `TestRecordAndBranch_AuditFAILRecordsFloorFailure`, `TestRecordAndBranch_AuditPASSDoesNotRecord`, `TestRecordAndBranch_NonAuthoritativePhaseFAILDoesNotRecord`).
- **`adviseOnUnclassifiedFailure` is the ADR-0044 C3 escalate→advise→promote hook.**
  - It runs only at `cfg.PhaseRecovery==enforce` on `ErrArtifactTimeout` aborts carrying a pane, checking the registry before consulting an LLM.
  - Every failure path WARNs without changing control flow; validated advice promotes through `recovery.PromoteAdvice`.
  - Pinned by `TestPhaseRecovery_ShadowDefault_NoCorrectiveAction`, `TestPhaseRecovery_Enforce_AdvisesAndPromotes`, `TestPhaseRecovery_Enforce_KnownPaneSkipsAdvisor` and `TestPhaseRecovery_Enforce_AdvisorErrorIsBestEffort`.
- **The hook's deterministic-first short-circuit strips agent-authored content from the pane before matching the registry.**
  - Matching raw panes misclassifies novel wedges quoting signatures in diff content.
  - It calls the bridge's C2 stripper with an empty prompt (`TestC1123_AgentDiffQuotedSignatureStillReachesAdvisor`, `TestC1123_BareDiffPrefixedSignatureStillReachesAdvisor`, `TestC1123_RealChromeStillSkipsAdvisor`, `TestC1123_AnchoredSeedUnderDiffLineStillSkipsAdvisor`).
- **The router's and epilogue's fallback fail-reason templates are the single source `isBoilerplateRouterReason` matches against** (`TestAgentGradedRouterReason_MatchesBoilerplateDetector`, `TestAssembleFailureDigest_BoilerplateOnlyReasonIsUnexplained`).
- **An `AbortReason` is not proof a cycle died there.** Ship recovery records recovered transients, so `backfillFailReasons` emits both abort entries and failing-phase markers (`TestBackfillFailReasons_RecoveredAbortDoesNotMaskRealFailure`).
- **`state.json`'s `failedApproaches` has two appenders with different Go types**: `FailedRecord` and `failurelog.Recorded`. A shape-parity test pins matching JSON keys (`TestFailedRecord_SupersetOfFailurelogRecordedShape`).
- **A git rename/copy dirties both old and new paths in `git status --porcelain`.** `porcelainDirtySet` records both so renamed deliverables never evade the tree-diff guard (`TestDefaultGitDirtyPaths_RenameEmitsBothSides`).
- **`gitCapture` returns stdout untrimmed and reports non-zero exits through exit code rather than error** (`TestGitCapture_RoutesThroughInjectedSeam`, `TestGitCapture_NonzeroExitReportedViaCode`).
- **`recoverBuildLeak`'s per-status disposition for source-writer phases (tdd/build).**
  - Untracked leaks relocate and stage; `go/evolve` and `go/bin/evolve` discard through `git checkout HEAD --`.
  - Tracked edits relocate when the worktree stays at HEAD, else worktree wins; add/delete/rename leaks discard or abort.
  - Unreadable `git status` degrades to true so the guard remains the backstop (`TestRecoverBuildLeak_*`).
- **A lane's recovery never claims a main-tree path keyed to an owner it does not hold.**
  - Live siblings or the mint registry keep foreign keyed paths in place (`TestRecoverBeforeReview_SiblingLaneEvalWrittenMidPhaseStaysInTheMainTree`).
  - Own leaked evals and unowned source leaks relocate; live own leases still fail the guard (`TestRecoverBeforeReview_OwnLaneEvalLeakStillRelocates`, `TestRecoverBeforeReview_SourceLeakStillRelocatesBesideASibling`, `TestRecoverBeforeReview_SiblingEvalDuringScoutStaysForItsOwner`, `TestRecoverBeforeReview_RegisteredMintStaysInTheMainTree`, `TestRecoverBeforeReview_SiblingEditToACommittedEvalIsNotStolen`, `TestGuardChargesThisLanesOwnEvalWhileItsOwnLeaseIsLive`).
  - Read-only phases discard rebuilt binaries; pinned by `TestMainTreeOwnership_*`, `TestLaneOwnership_*`, and `TestLiveSiblingsOf_ReadsOnlyLiveRunsOtherThanThisLane`.
- **The scout's eval home is its workspace, and a main-tree copy of its own eval does not stay in the main tree.** Recovery moves it into the lane worktree before review (`TestRecoverBeforeReview_ScoutsOwnMainTreeEvalCopyLeavesTheMainTree`, `TestGuardIgnoresScoutEvalMaterialization`).
- **A phase's recovery never claims what was dirty when the phase started**, on fresh and resume paths (`TestRunCycle_PhaseBaselineExcludesASiblingWriteBeforeThePhase`, `TestRunCycleFromPhase_PhaseBaselineExcludesASiblingWriteBeforeThePhase`, `TestMaybeRemediate_FixDispatchBaselineExcludesASiblingWriteDuringTheGate`).
- **`evolveDeliverablePrefixes` marks `.evolve/` subpaths that are repo deliverables** (`evals/`, `phases/`, `commit-prefix-scope.json`). Runtime state and operator files (`.evolve/profiles/`, `.evolve/policy.json`) never relocate (`TestRecoverBuildLeak_SkipsEvolveRuntimeStateAndNestedWorktreeDir`, `TestRecoverBuildLeak_SkipsNestedEvolveRuntimeState`).
- **`preserveOnVerdict` keeps a normally-completed cycle's worktree when `FinalVerdict` is FAIL.** PASS, `SHIPPED_VIA_BUILD`, and `SKIPPED_UNKNOWN` cycles prune normally (`TestPreserveOnVerdict`).
- **`LeakRecoverablePhase` and `WorktreePhase` are deliberately separate predicates.** `WorktreePhase` gates write permission for the role gate. It holds for the source writers of `phasecontract.SourceWriterPhases`. `LeakRecoverablePhase` holds for every phase that runs with an active cycle worktree: triage, audit, scout, tdd, build and `bug-reproduction`. Thus leak recovery reaches all of them without a wider write permission, and `bug-reproduction` is in both sets since 2026-10-20. Pinned by `TestLeakRecoverablePhase_CoversAllWorktreePhases`, `TestLeakRecoverablePhase_ExcludesPhasesWithoutAnActiveWorktree` and `TestWorktreePhase_UnchangedByRecoveryDecoupling`.
- **Every correction-ladder rung emits one `gate.corrected` INFO signal** (`CodeGateCorrection`) on fresh and resume paths, naming ordinal, budget, rung, and CLI escalation (`TestReviewWithCorrections_EmitsGateCorrectedPerRedispatch`, `TestReviewWithCorrections_EscalatedRedispatchSaysSo`, `TestResumeReviewGate_EmitsGateCorrectedOnTheResumeRoot`).
- **`Next`'s verdict branch resolves through catalog `OnPass`/`OnFail` before the literal switch** (ADR-0058). Bad targets fail with `ErrTransitionInvalid`; missing catalogs fall back to the literal table (`TestTransitionKernelOracle_Next`, `TestNext_AuditVerdictBranchFromSpec`, `TestNext_AuditDegradesToLiteralWhenCatalogUnset`, `TestWiredStateMachine_ReproducesAuditOracle`, `TestNext_UnresolvableTargetErrorsLoudly`).
- **`gateSatisfied`'s artifact-floor thresholds are config-driven** (ADR-0060). Catalog `verdict_in` sets choose passing verdicts; absent handoffs never satisfy, and missing catalogs keep literal thresholds (`TestGateSatisfied_ConfigThresholds`, `TestGateSatisfied_DegradesToLiteral`).
- **Every `agent_subprocess` ledger writer must stamp `run_id`, enforced by a closed-set source scan.**
  - Ship and composition reject auditor rows without `run_id`; `agentSubprocessWriters` lists all allowed writers, and `centrallyStamped` self-prunes.
  - Line-level scans require `core.RunIDFromWorkspace` calls alongside behavioral tests (`subagent/runid_stamp_test.go`, `cyclesimulator/runid_stamp_test.go`, `core/phase_bindings_fail_verdict_test.go`).
  - Pinned by `TestAgentSubprocessWriters_AllStampRunID` and `TestAgentSubprocessWriters_SetIsClosed`.
- **`LedgerEntry.Source` attributes skipped phases (`psmas`, `router`, `content`).** `recordRoutingDecision` stamps `Source:"router"` on `phase_skipped` rows, and `omitempty` keeps old hashes stable (`TestLedgerEntrySource_*`).
- **The debugger of a fleet-rebase conflict may write exactly the conflicted paths.** `rebaseWithDerivedRegen` returns the non-derived paths it met in conflict. Derived projections are regenerated, never handed on.
  - `rebaseRecordingConflicts` is the one writer of `CycleState.ShipRecoveryConflicts`. The ship latch clears it with `ShipRecoveryCode`.
  - `worktreeWritablePaths` hands the paths to the debugger alone, and only while the recovery is a fleet rebase. `fenceFor` puts them in `Fence.Writable`.
  - `unmergedRebasePaths` reads with `-z`, so a conflicted path is the raw path that the fence compares, never git's quoted form. Pinned by `TestRebaseCycleBranchOntoMain_NamesAConflictedFileAsTheFenceSeesIt` (integration tier).
  - Pinned also by `TestRebaseWithDerivedRegen_NamesEveryNonDerivedConflictAndNoDerivedOne`, `TestRebaseRecordingConflicts_TheCycleRemembersWhatItsDebuggerMayResolve` (integration tier), `TestLatchShippedState_ForgetsTheConflictsOfTheRecoveryThatLanded`, `TestWorktreeWritablePaths_OnlyTheDebuggerOfAFleetRebaseMayWriteItsConflicts`, `TestResumePath_TheDebuggerOfAFleetRebaseMayWriteItsConflicts` and `TestRecoverFromShipError_TheFenceKeepsTheDebuggersResolutionOfTheConflictedFile`.
- **The carry compares the base the audited worktree stood on, never main's HEAD at audit time.**
  - `recordAuditBinding` writes `CycleState.WorktreeBaseSHA`; `carriedAudit` reads `auditledger.Entry.AuditedBase` and declines rows without a base.
  - Pinned by `TestRouteRebasedExplanation_AMainThatMovedBeforeTheAuditStillCarries`, `TestCarriedAudit_ARowNamingNoBaseNeverCarriesEvenOnAnEmptyBase`, `TestEmitPhaseBindings_TheAuditRowNamesTheBaseTheAuditedWorktreeStoodOn` and `TestEntry_AuditedBasePrefersTheWorktreeBaseAndFallsBackToTheHead`.
- **Every `LedgerEntry` field survives the JSON round trip.** `UnmarshalJSON` copies every `ledgerEntryWire` field by hand (`TestLedgerEntry_EveryFieldSurvivesTheLedgerRoundTrip`).
- **Null `cycle` values read as absent, and numeric `cycle` values fit int32 bounds.** History holds `"cycle":null` rows, and int32 keeps 32-bit and 64-bit builds equal (`TestLedgerEntry_NullCycleAbsorbed`, `TestLedgerEntry_Unmarshal_*`).
- **The auditor row's exit code is the host's disposition, never the report's.** `auditBindingExitCode` maps PASS to 0, WARN to 1, and FAIL to 2 (`TestEmitPhaseBindings_AuditFAIL_RecordsBinding_NoCachePut`, `TestEmitPhaseBindings_AuditBuild_CorrectMapping`).
- **The legality graph is config-driven and validated at load as a hard gate.**
  - `legalGraphFrom(config.LegalSuccessors)` builds the graph; `ValidateSafetyInvariants` rejects graphs stranding ship, and `NewOrchestrator` returns `ErrUnsafeConfig`.
  - Pinned by `TestLegalGraph_ConfigMatchesLiteral`, `TestWithLegalGraph_EmptyDegradesToLiteral`, `TestValidateSafetyInvariants_ConfigGraphStrandsShip` and `TestOrchestrator_UnsafeLegalGraphFailsClosed`.
- **The lost-landing floor's verdict is WARN, chosen deliberately over PASS or FAIL.**
  - Audited builds are not broken, while WARN excludes cycles from `IsShippingVerdict` without distorting quality metrics.
  - Detection keys on shipping claims with `ship-error.json` and no `ship-binding.json`, ignoring git HEAD movement in fleet mode.
  - Empty workspace paths fail closed without reading CWD, and the signal never halts the batch.
  - Pinned by `TestDetectLostLanding_*`, `TestLostLandingVerdict_IsLegalAndNotShipping`, `TestFinalizeCycle_LostLandingDowngradesTheVerdictAndRecordsTheSignal` and `TestFinalizeCycle_LandedCycleIsUnchanged`.
- **`mandatoryAnchorsFor` derives the spine-anchor order from config (`cfg.Order` ∩ `cfg.Mandatory`).**
  - Empty orders keep the canonical order; `SpineSatisfiedUpTo` walks anchor indices so non-evaluator anchors cannot weaken ship floors.
  - Pinned by `TestMandatoryAnchorsFor_*`, `TestSpineSatisfiedUpTo_NonAnchorIsUnconstrained` and `TestSpineSatisfiedUpTo_TriageMandatoryDoesNotWeakenFloor`.
- **`recordJudgmentLesson` yields when its phase is authoritative.**
  - Authoritative phases already record `FailedRecord`, preventing P1 todos from shadowing halts.
  - Lessons file at P1 with `IntentRejected` TTL and persist immediately (`TestJudgmentLesson_*`).
